Activity callback requires the lease profile to remain exactly equal to that snapshot, in addition to provider pin/activity checks. Use the owned snapshot for source-budget parsing and repeat checks during loading and before/after yield/context exit. A nested scratch-budget mutation during input loading fails before invoking the installed pipeline and unwinds source mappings.


### Region execution profile compiler

`v2region.CompiledRegionProfiles` implements RegionProfileProvider using mandatory installation-owned RegionCostQualifier. Check cancellation before qualification and after its return; verify FrozenRegionPlan.SourceShape before invoking dependencies. Pass an owned copy of the frozen body/input inventory. Qualifier must verify installed runtime/device/model/ICC qualification and resolve the entire effective StyleV2 including nested/adaptive operator costs. Missing qualifier returns REGION_PREVIEW_UNAVAILABLE; unresolved/unqualified required operators must fail rather than return default costs. A concrete native/registry qualifier remains required for default installation.

`CompileRegionExecutionPolicy(plan,cost)` requires canonical lowercase runtime/policy/model/ICC SHA256 pins, exact frozen recipe model/ICC agreement, nonnegative safe-integer operator costs and positive qualified CUDA VRAM peak. It freezes cuda/v2.2/normalize_uint16 profile fields. SourceResourceBudget uses full verified canvas dimensions; add retained float RGBA canvas (16 bytes per full-source pixel), conservative presentation temporary allowance (64 bytes per output pixel), minimum 3GiB StyleV2 parsing RAM, qualified operator/model RAM. Scratch adds retained canvas, output staging (5 bytes per ROI pixel plus2MiB), bounded encoded derivative16MiB and JSON manifest8MiB, plus qualified operator scratch. Output budget/reserved quota is24MiB plus qualified additional output cost. Reject any resulting estimate exceeding 2^53−1. Qualifier cost semantics exclude these orchestration costs and include all operator/model peaks and simultaneously live intermediates. Syntax/cost compilation alone does not prove qualification or activate SQL catalog authority. Queue still rechecks live access/policy/quota during admission.

PinnedRegionRenderer snapshots verify_runtime/admit_resources/render callbacks and invokes the captured callbacks. All existing provider pin checks also require callback identity: ordinary Python bound methods compare function/receiver identity, other callables compare object identity. Callback substitution before execution or during context lifetime fails RUNTIME_LOCK_MISMATCH and unwinds the original context. This is cooperative consistency protection, not isolation from arbitrary native code.


### Pinned Region calibration catalog

`RegionWorkloadSHA(plan)` hashes immutable base-style Ref, effective correction, renderer, model/ICC contract after checking the frozen source proof. Exclude source identity and ROI so exact effective-style workload qualification can be reused across measured source dimensions; adaptive/nested operator behavior must be included by the independent measurement evidence. A single favorable image measurement is insufficient to claim a general workload bound.

`NewPinnedRegionCalibrationCatalog(raw,externalSHA)` owns canonical deployment manifest schema_version1 with1–128 RegionCalibration profiles. Exact qualification fields are runtime_lock_sha/model_lock_sha/icc_lock_sha/device_profile_sha/dataset_sha/policy_sha/backend(cuda). Each profile binds workload_sha/evidence_sha and1–256 samples; sample fields width/height/operator_peak and explicit ram_bytes/scratch_bytes/vram_bytes/output_bytes are mandatory, including explicit zeros. Reject null/missing/alias/unknown fields, duplicate keys via canonical parsing, duplicate qualification+workload profiles, duplicate dimensions, invalid source dimensions, negative/unsafe costs, and zero CUDA VRAM. Digest equality proves installed bytes only; operator deployment must independently approve physically qualified measurements and their complete workload coverage.

`LoadPinnedRegionCalibrationCatalog(path,externalSHA)` requires an absolute path, bounds regular-file reads to1MiB, rejects symlinks/hardlinks/FIFOs/devices, and verifies size/mtime across reading plus authoritative content hash. Catalog owns decoded sample storage. Atomic replacement applies to newly loaded catalogs; live SQL authority remains separately mandatory.

`Select(ctx,qualification,plan)` requires exact qualification tuple and workload, matching frozen model/ICC pins, and a measured source sample covering both full-canvas axes without rotation/extrapolation. Choose smallest covering area then smallest width, independent of registry order. Retain all operator RAM/scratch/VRAM/output peaks unchanged; no pixel-ratio downscaling. Missing coverage returns REGION_PREVIEW_UNAVAILABLE. `CalibratedRegionCosts` implements RegionCostQualifier using mandatory live RegionQualificationResolver and pinned catalog; validate plan before dependencies, pass owned plan slices, preserve cancellation after resolver via Select. RegionQualificationResolver implementation must enforce tenant/project scope, operation/policy activation, trusted passed ready report, exact installed pins/calibration evidence and license clearance. Concrete SQL resolver and activation/adoption enforcement remain required; this catalog does not synthesize them or reuse Render qualification.


### Live Region qualification resolution

Forward-only migration00455 adds nullable DBA-owned region_icc_lock_sha and region_execution_policy_sha pins to v2_qualification_policies; no policy/operation is activated. photo.resolve_v26_region_qualification(tenant,project,calibrationSHA,evidenceSHA) is SECURITY DEFINER with fixed search_path, photo_fn_owner ownership and EXECUTE only for photo_api. Require matching transaction tenant, active tenant/project, enabled CUDA region.preview@1 operation, the selected enabled/licensed admission policy, explicit Region ICC/execution policy pins and membership of actual deployment calibration/evidence SHA in policy allowlists. Require a trusted passed tenant/project report matching exact operation/version/model/runtime/backend/device/dataset whose artifact is ready, verified, version-pinned and unexpired. No matching row means REGION_PREVIEW_UNAVAILABLE; metadata resolution is not physical qualification.

SQLRegionQualificationResolver owns deployment calibration/evidence pins, verifies frozen source proof and tenant/project identity, runs under photo_api tenant transaction, checks returned model/ICC against frozen recipe and preserves cancellation. Queue preparation retains separate editor authorization. NewSQLRegionProfileProvider(api,catalog,evidenceSHA) binds resolver calibration SHA to the actual internally retained immutable catalog manifest digest; require every bundled profile to share the explicit approved evidence SHA. Mixed-evidence bundles require separate providers. Constructor performs no IO or activation; every compile resolves live SQL authority. Freeze/recheck the entire selected calibration tuple at seal/worker/adoption remains mandatory before default operation activation.

Region output reservation uses the authoritative catalog maximums:16MiB derivative plus8MiB JSON document, totaling24MiB before qualified extra output costs. This supersedes the earlier1MiB document assumption; scratch staging allowance increases accordingly.


### Frozen Region measured qualification projection

RegionQualifiedCost now requires a value RegionCalibrationPin with exact live qualification tuple, catalog_sha, calibration_sha (canonical entire RegionCalibration profile hash), workload_sha, evidence_sha and the selected width/height/operator_peak sample. Catalog.Select computes this projection from owned installed calibration records; caller-provided cost overrides must match every selected peak. CompileRegionExecutionPolicy requires matching runtime/model/ICC/policy tuple, valid independent pins, exact frozen workload hash and covering measured sample. Emit the complete pin as region_qualification. No omission/default pin is accepted by this compiler.

FrozenRegionPlan.CompileNode validates a present region_qualification with exact nested object fields and reconstructs the compiler-owned profile projection. Require exact runtime/alpha/resource/pin values before input closure resolution or Params publication; preserve additional installer-owned profile fields. Canonical whole-object JSON fragments are compared directly because the installed canonical library only accepts object/array roots. Existing self-mapping uncalibrated provider profiles retain their preceding contract until mandatory activation migration; this compatibility path does not establish qualification or readiness for default Region activation.

Forward-only00456 introduces private v26_region_qualification_pin_ready(tenant,project,policySHA,profile), rejecting malformed complete tuple/pins/sample, noninteger unsafe measured peaks, zero CUDA VRAM or disagreement with resource_estimate.vram_bytes. Lock project/operation/selected policy and all exact trusted candidate qualification/report rows through caller transaction; require actual catalog/evidence allowlisting and exact equality with current resolve_v26_region_qualification result. Renamed seal/adoption predecessors are private and service EXECUTE is revoked. Existing seal scope validates persisted immutable profile equality and authority first; calibrated profiles then pass live qualification gate. Worker admission already delegates to seal scope. Adoption wrapper locks tenant/run/task and checks calibrated Region pin before prior full adoption chain. Preserve preceding uncalibrated profile behavior; default operation remains disabled. Mandatory pins for every activation, worker-side independent catalog membership/profile-hash verification and full default native/API activation remain required before declaring Region fully installed.


### Independent worker Region calibration verification

operations/region/calibration.py defines strict Qualification/Peak/Sample/Calibration/Manifest/CalibrationPin matching the server wire contract. Installed PinnedRegionCalibrationCatalog owns canonical immutable bytes, validates external SHA, exact fields/integer costs, unique tuples/samples, source bounds and positive CUDA VRAM. Optional file-backed installation requires absolute regular single-link NOFOLLOW/NONBLOCK bounded1MiB reads, size/mtime consistency; recheck installed bytes during use. Include calibration.py in RegionRuntimeLock exact component inventory. Loading calibration metadata does not physically qualify CUDA/model/operators.

Catalog.admit(profile,params,source=None) independently checks catalog membership, complete calibration profile hash, exact measured sample/evidence/workload, frozen model/ICC and runtime/backend/alpha fields. Resource values must be exact nonnegative safe integers, RAM meets3GiB parser floor and VRAM equals measured peak. With verified WorkingImage metadata, deterministically select covering full-canvas sample and independently recompute complete server RAM/scratch/VRAM/output budget, including24MiB output cap and alpha normalization. Reject substituted samples, extrapolation and budget changes before source-pixel mapping/native pipeline. Workload hash mirrors Go base-style/correction/renderer/model/ICC exactly.

PinnedRegionRenderer accepts optional installation-owned calibration_catalog of the exact pinned catalog type. Calibrated installations require provider.device_profile_sha canonical SHA256, retained as an immutable provider pin; task qualification device SHA must match it before provider resource admission/native rendering. Provider qualification must independently prove that actual installed hardware identity. Calibrated profiles without an installed catalog fail REGION_CALIBRATION_UNAVAILABLE. During admission check membership before delegating operator budgets; at render check metadata/selection/full budget before loading pixels or entering provider context. Freeze complete lease profile in both mapped and calibrated self-mapping modes; recheck file-backed catalog in runtime verification. Existing uncalibrated provider path remains pending mandatory all-activation pin migration; default installation remains incomplete.


### Mandatory measured proof for newly queued Region tasks

QueueService validates provider execution policy with validateQueuedRegionPolicy before input resolution, Params publication or snapshot freeze. Own/bound/canonicalize profile bytes, require region_qualification (missing => REGION_PREVIEW_UNAVAILABLE), validate exact pin/compiler-owned projection and full-source/artifact budgets. PolicySHA must equal retained qualification.policy_sha and ReservedBytes must equal resource_estimate.output_bytes exactly; malformed proof or changed quota/policy returns REGION_PREVIEW_INVALID without partial profile. Every new task through QueueService requires this contract. Lower-level legacy CompileNode/SQL compatibility paths remain pending mandatory database activation enforcement; this queue gate alone does not close privileged direct SQL dispatch.


### Mandatory Region qualification at every activation entry point

Forward-only00457 removes uncalibrated compatibility in v26_region_seal_scope and adopt_v2_plan: absent/null proof cannot seal, independently pass worker admission or publish adopted Region output. Existing profiles must be requalified before those actions; this migration does not enable Region. Lower-level Go CompileNode also requires measured proof before dependencies; controlled fixtures now use compiled calibration profiles. Historical descriptor reads retain their separate persisted-source/live-access contract.

Forward-only00458 wraps generic scheduler seal_v2_graph, revoking service access to its renamed predecessor. Any Region config or task triggers tenant→usage→run locking and requires exactly one Region node/task plus full v26_region_seal_scope, including current qualification and authority. Only then delegate existing generic graph checks. Other operation paths retain the preceding implementation. This closes direct generic SQL graph activation that bypassed the Go Region-specific seal call. Default operation remains disabled pending physical native/model/CUDA qualification and complete runtime/API/client installation.


### Region API and scheduler database boundaries

QueueService now requires separate DB (API preparation/availability/snapshot) and Scheduler (draft/input registration/seal/ticket) pools. node.Admit must run under photo_scheduler and checks current_user before admission. Region BeginInTransaction/SealInTransaction call the narrow scheduler authority functions introduced by forward-only00459. v26_region_admission_scope_scheduler and v26_region_seal_scope_scheduler are fixed-search-path SECURITY DEFINER photo_fn_owner functions delegating full original actor/session/snapshot/run/recipe/calibration authority; EXECUTE only photo_scheduler. Original API authority functions retain their preceding API-only grants. No service receives direct table grants or access to private predecessors.

API installation must construct request-bound source preparation/input catalog/closure publisher under API and a distinct scheduler pool. Existing QueueService previously could not complete SQL admission using one role: begin/input registration/seal/ticket require scheduler while snapshot/availability/publication require API. This boundary correction is necessary but does not itself mount Region in default API startup. No operation activation/physical qualification is implied.


### Request-scoped Region preparation without object IO in SQL transactions

RegionPreparation abstracts QueueService preparation. SQLRegionPreparation owns API pool/object reader/bucket, validates identity/dependencies/cancellation, reads source and recipe metadata under transaction-local tenant scope and actual photo_api role, then commits before any S3 read. Service.preparationMetadata validates public request, exact recipe ref/project binding and authorized pinned metadata; Service.finishPreparation loads pinned immutable objects, proves source/recipe/canvas and freezes Region plan after transaction release. Original Service.Prepare remains for already scoped/autocommit readers. Cancellation between SQL and object loading is checked. No metadata read authorizes dispatch by itself; admission rechecks live authority. Invalid/foreign source metadata fails before object IO.


### Request-bound Region dependencies and API installation

SQLRegionDependencies.ForRegionPlan builds a fresh ClosureResolver/SQLArtifactCatalog bound to frozen project/asset-version and API pool, API v2draft.Publisher for immutable Params, and scheduler artifactjson.Publisher/ScopedPublisher bound to tenant/run for input closure. Require distinct nonnil API/scheduler pools, objects/bucket/run and verified plan source shape. QueueService invokes the factory only after complete measured profile validation and rejects missing returned dependencies before IO. No tenant/project/version/run state is reused between requests.

photo API configuration supports all-or-none PHOTO_REGION_CALIBRATION_FILE (absolute), PHOTO_REGION_CALIBRATION_SHA256 and PHOTO_REGION_EVIDENCE_SHA256 (canonical digests). Before startup load independently pinned catalog; buildRegionAdmission requires complete Region SQL boundary, approved common evidence, actual API role and separate scheduler pool (MaxConns8, MinConns0, statement_timeout30s). Mount QueueService on httpapi.Server.RegionQueue with SQLRegionPreparation, live calibrated profiles and request-bound dependency factory. Missing catalog leaves queue unavailable; invalid configured catalog/evidence/schema/roles fails startup. This installation does not enable operation registry or physically qualify GPU/native provider. deploy/compose.region-calibration.yaml provides optional read-only non-creating file mount and required catalog/evidence digests.

Region Queue must bind the prepared frozen plan to the invoking tenant and asset and to the complete canonical RegionPreviewRequest immediately after preparation and before qualification or publication. Reject substituted version, recipe, ROI or output size even when the substituted plan is internally hash-valid. SQL admission independently rechecks actor/session and live access.

Render native background blur uses render_blur_params to materialize explicit background.blur_strength with caller-verified source/subject/depth refs. Do not rescale that value with global color strength. prepare_blur_stage retains float alpha/generated owners and measured depth/radius evidence; low subject confidence forces review downstream. Runtime locks include shared native background/geometry code; any such byte change requires regenerated independently qualified locks before operation activation.

FrozenBackgroundAsset is the canonical imported-background normalization pin. Fields: schema_version=1, portable_asset_id (nonempty UTF-8 up to120 bytes, no surrounding whitespace), nonzero style_version_id, exact accepted-style ArtifactRef style, imported ArtifactRef source, expected_bytes1..536870912, media_type JPEG/PNG/TIFF with exact suffix .jpg/.png/.tiff respectively, explicit nullable assume_profile from supported ICC registry IDs and work_icc_sha. Decode rejects unknown/noncanonical fields, zero identities and unsupported pairs. Resolve under active tenant/project editor authority, require accepted style selecting background.mode=asset and exact asset_id, ready verified versioned unexpired objects. Native normalization validates source bytes/magic/suffix and target ICC and never flattens alpha. Recipe membership and SQL admission authority must be installed before enabling authored asset mode.

Migration00460 permits optional inline background_asset in registered FrozenRenderRecipe and persists its exact JSON pin. Private v26_render_background_binding_ready repeats accepted author-selected style/version/project and imported/config object readiness under row locks. Recipe/scalars base_style and icc_lock_sha must match pin; replay cannot change it. The helper has no service-role EXECUTE grants. Registration alone is not task admission or qualification: closure/source authorization, live seal/worker/adoption checks and native resource qualification remain required.

#### Imported-background native assembly binding

`prepare_portrait_background_base(..., normalized_background=...)` accepts `asset` only with `inputs.recipe.background_asset` and `NormalizedBackground` from the live `normalize_frozen_background` context. The caller admits decode resources before opening that context and retains its owners until composition exits. Assembly validates the effective module's portable ID against the frozen pin (including whole-module overrides), pin/base-style identity, source SHA and working ICC, opaque bounded metadata and readonly float32 pixel shape. Composition uses the imported source ArtifactRef in ComposeParams, scale=1, center=(.5,.5), shadow_opacity=0; these defaults are the complete geometry supported by the authored Background contract. Color/beauty precede background replacement; original protected RGB/alpha is restored last. Retained normalized bytes are included in portrait_background_peak; this estimate does not establish physical decoder/runtime qualification. Generate mode continues to require its separate complete pipeline. Default factory and transport/adoption wiring must not be considered implemented by this helper.

`render_frozen_compose_params(prepared_style, frozen, subject, tile_size=...)` derives compose image/source refs from a canonical owned FrozenRenderRecipe. Asset mode requires the effective background portable ID to match the pin; callers must use the fully merged effective style, including whole-module overrides. Ordered assembly separately checks the resulting image ref against the frozen recipe source before allocating outputs, including blur mode. This function establishes parameter identity only; SQL live scope, admitted normalization, approved runtime and independent adoption remain separate requirements.

#### Mandatory imported-background decoder admission

`normalize_background_asset` and `normalize_frozen_background` require keyword-only `execution_profile` and `retained_resources`. Retained resources contain exactly nonnegative safe-integer `ram_bytes` and `scratch_bytes` for all simultaneously owned allocations other than this imported source/decoder. They must include source/color/beauty/protection owners when normalization overlaps those stages. Budgets are copied before callbacks/IO. Invalid budgets fail before input access; compressed source bytes are admitted before download, then actual header dimensions determine `background_decode_peak` and are admitted before native ICC decode or output allocation. The peak counts full float32 RGB mapping, compressed source, the existing ingest decoder's 1GiB native baseline and 128 bytes per tile pixel; scratch counts compressed source and RGB mapping/header allowance. This static bound does not replace physical runtime qualification or full Render resource admission; installer must include the decoder peak alongside retained composition costs, never treat them as mutually exclusive without evidence.

#### Frozen asset background assembly context

`prepare_frozen_asset_background_base` joins immutable background parameter materialization, mandatory admitted normalization and ordered native color/beauty/background composition. It requires FrozenRenderInputs, the fully effective style, registry, verified subject metadata/pixels and explicit retained-resource costs. It snapshots recipe/style/source metadata and budgets before input callbacks, rejects source canvas/ICC/dtype or subject geometry/confidence mismatch before download, derives ComposeParams from the owned frozen recipe, and retains normalize_frozen_background's context through the full render yield. Consumers must stage outputs before exiting. This context supports the declared portrait/background module subset only and is not the complete default worker factory: remaining authored operators, complete graph authority, quality measurement, report publication and independent adoption are still mandatory downstream/upstream integrations.

#### SQL background task inventory gate (migration00462)

Private `v26_render_direct_inputs_ready` retains prior direct source/style/session/override checks and additionally binds any `background_asset` to the task params artifact's exact stored registered pin in that tenant/project. It requires the matching source ArtifactRef/SHA as `StyleBackgroundAsset` in `v2_task_inputs` and live accepted background binding readiness. Missing, substituted or omitted stored pins fail; an untyped `Artifact` entry cannot satisfy the typed dependency. The helper remains SECURITY DEFINER with fixed search_path and no API/public EXECUTE. Existing full Render worker/seal/adoption gates call it internally; this migration does not activate operators or grant direct table access.

#### Native authored base callback

`NativePortraitBackgroundBase` is a task-local callable matching authored Render's `(inputs, PreparedRenderStyle, original, masks)` boundary. Provider installers can supply it as `render_base` with `supported_modules=provider.supported_modules` to ProfiledTaskRenderer's loader. It routes absent background to color/beauty, keep/blur/solid/transparent to ordered composition, and asset to admitted frozen import normalization/composition. `load_evidence` must be a qualified scoped context returning `NativeBaseEvidence`; it proves source/model identity and owns faces/person/region/subject/depth arrays throughout the render yield. Resource budgets and effective style are copied; unsupported modules/generate mode fail before the loader. The provider does not supply model qualification, fabricate quality metrics or publish reports. Geometry, generative/restoration/detail/defect pipelines and full default CLI installer remain required separately. The new provider module is included in the installed Render/Region runtime component inventory; do not reuse a previous runtime approval after installing it.

`load_pinned_background_arrays` loads subject MaskRef and optional RelativeDepth documents through TaskArtifacts, owns canonical metadata, validates the exact source canvas/transform, subject selector/confidence/protection flags and complete depth, and maps readonly float32 unit arrays inside one ExitStack. Mandatory sealed RAM/scratch budgets include already-retained resources; mapped/downloaded analysis arrays and allowances are admitted before metadata/pixel IO. Caller must select adopted analysis document refs and prove model/source provenance; geometry and closure membership alone cannot confer analysis authority. The yielded tuple can populate NativeBaseEvidence while this scope remains open. No implicit segmentation, invalid-depth filling, mask substitution or assumption of review approval is allowed.

NativePortraitBackgroundBase snapshots task input metadata and effective style before analysis loading, and passes a separate metadata copy to load_evidence. Mutations by that callback cannot replace the frozen selection used for rendering or alter caller-owned recipe/style. Original pixel/mask mappings remain shared scoped readonly owners; the callback must not outlive their context. Cancellation checks during normalization/composition and after render yield unwind every analysis/import/array/scratch owner; no staged output may be committed after cancellation.

`discover_pinned_background_refs` performs bounded typed discovery through `TaskArtifacts.declared_contract_refs` after metadata RAM admission. Exactly one unprotected person/subject MaskRef with confidence >=.75 and exact source canvas/transform is required. Blur additionally requires exactly one complete same-canvas RelativeDepth; other compose modes do not discover depth. Missing or ambiguous candidates fail rather than choosing by inventory order, UUID or selector preference. The returned refs feed load_pinned_background_arrays inside the qualified analysis loader. Closure membership and same-canvas geometry are insufficient to qualify model/source provenance. Current frozen full-Render metadata does not itself add mandatory analysis roots: installer/planner must still supply the explicitly adopted dependencies or compute qualified scoped analysis before enabling the full pipeline.

ProfiledTaskRenderer snapshots admitted task input metadata before invoking load_providers and passes a separate metadata copy to that callback. It renders from its retained snapshot, not callback-mutated source/recipe metadata. Execution profile comes from the authoritative SQL RenderAdoptionInputs snapshot; a callback change to ctx.lease.execution_profile is rejected with CONFIG_INVALID before authored execution. This ownership guard supplements SQL live qualification/closure/fencing; it is not permission for the loader to alter task context or runtime pins.

PinnedRegionRenderer owns Region params/source/effective-style metadata before calling provider code; resource-admission callbacks receive an owned params copy. Parameter or execution-profile changes are rejected with CONFIG_INVALID before output yield. Profiles are snapshotted even for direct/unmapped providers. The mapped full-canvas path keeps its existing specific REGION_EFFECTIVE_STYLE_CHANGED / REGION_SOURCE_METADATA_CHANGED guards; it receives logically identical independent metadata and retains pixel owners through context exit. Provider model/runtime/callback identity checks and calibration qualification remain separate mandatory guards. Cancellation/error must close provider contexts before final staging.

`prepare_defect_stage` owns and canonically revalidates WorkingImage, DefectCandidates and DefectReview before artifact callbacks, snapshots reference identities and execution profile, and computes accepted selection from those owned values. Every declared document must still match those exact bytes when loaded through TaskArtifacts. A callback change to the review's accepted list cannot silently revoke/add candidates after selection while comparing the same aliased model to itself; it fails RENDER_DEFECT_DOCUMENT_CHANGED before native repair. Pixel/protection/label-map comparisons and review source/revision/identity guards remain required. This guard does not approve review or permit applying a proposal to post-color/beauty pixels without exact-image binding.

`prepare_pinned_defect_repair(artifacts, params_ref, current, protection_ref=..., execution_profile=...)` is the scoped context for declared DefectRepairParams. It owns Params/review/candidates/source/resolved metadata, verifies explicit protection document equals inline Params protection, checks resolved source color/source SHA, rejects STYLE_OOD_SKIP, resolves defect strength with existing resolve_effective_params and admits label/protection owners before pixel mapping. Exact reviewed current RGB/alpha and accepted candidate binding are checked by prepare_defect_stage. Disabled resolved module uses zero-strength repair while preserving source checks/outcomes; it cannot bypass review binding. Returned candidate outcomes and inherited resolved needs_review must be retained by the render_proposal installer and final measurement, never reduced to pixels with discarded review evidence. This helper does not itself install an operator, publish a report or qualify runtime.


#### Scoped defect layer outcomes

`PinnedDefectLayerProposal` is a task-local `render_proposal(item, current)` context for `defects.repair@1`. It owns LoadedRenderLayer metadata before callbacks, checks the exact runtime SHA and canonical declared DefectRepairParams, and retains prepare_pinned_defect_repair owners through yield. It returns RenderLayerProposal with pure RenderLayerEffect metadata bound to layer UUID, operation/version and runtime. Composition validates this binding before blending and retains outcomes in AdjustmentPixels.effects; PreparedRender combines them with base outcomes as RenderCombinedEffects. Publication's existing effect_review_required guard therefore retains skipped/protected/empty-candidate and inherited review requirements. All outcomes are validated even after an earlier review requirement; duplicate layer UUIDs fail. Pixel mappings from expired proposal contexts are not retained in outcome records. This callback still requires explicit qualified installation and exact reviewed-current-image binding; it does not complete the default factory or full transport/adoption path.

Active defects.repair layers must return RenderLayerProposal; raw AdjustmentPixels fail RENDER_LAYER_EFFECT_REQUIRED before blend allocation. compose_render_adjustments snapshots the ordered layer inventory and supplies independent metadata copies to proposal callbacks. Outcome binding is checked against its retained snapshot, so callback mutation of layer identity/operation/runtime cannot redefine the expected binding. Disabled/zero-opacity layers remain skipped after inventory qualification.


#### Bound layer provider inventory

`RenderLayerProviders(providers, check_active=...)` accepts a bounded tuple of RenderLayerProvider(operator, prepare). Each operator supplies its Params contract, source binding validator, runtime SHA and qualification callback. Duplicate operation/version, malformed identifiers/runtime, invalid contract types and non-callable handlers fail at construction. Its operators tuple feeds load_render_layer_params; the same registry is render_proposal. Dispatch owns LoadedRenderLayer metadata, requires exact operation/version/runtime and Params model, re-verifies runtime immediately before provider execution, and retains the provider context through yield/cancellation. It has no unknown-operation fallback. ProfiledTaskRenderer requires an installed registry's operators to equal the loader's Params inventory. Legacy explicit callbacks remain supported for existing qualified installations; this registry does not qualify runtime itself or install missing native handlers. render_providers is included in the composition runtime hash inventory.


#### Shared native portrait detail preparation

`operations.portrait.prepare_detail.prepare_detail` accepts float32 unit RGB, explicit confirmed DetailRegion contracts, enabled PortraitDetail settings, a scoped mask loader and float32 protection. It owns and canonically revalidates metadata before callbacks; finite/unit arrays are checked in row tiles. Regions retain existing lexicographic region_id order, strength selection and apply_region linear-light math. Teeth/sclera chroma/luminance, shine and under-eye/cloth adjustments remain unchanged; glasses remains review_required. It yields readonly RGB and typed DetailEvidence with cancellation checks before/after yield. Source pixels/alpha are never modified; consumer must retain mask owners and preserve alpha separately.

Standalone portrait.detail now uses this preparation context before existing staging/report publication. `detail_peak(width,height)` counts conservative full-frame native memory (160 bytes/pixel plus 1MiB, minimum 1GiB) and float32 RGB scratch. Enabled standalone execution rejects insufficient RAM before pixel IO. This static estimate is not physical qualification. Runtime inventory includes preparation, native algorithm and contracts. Render installation still requires exact reviewed/source mask binding, admitted retained resources, typed detail outcomes and final quality/adoption integration; extraction alone does not enable that module in the authored full renderer.


#### Pinned Render portrait detail stage

`prepare_pinned_detail(artifacts, params_ref, current, execution_profile=..., check_active=...)` owns PortraitParams/WorkingImage/ResolvedStyle before native execution. It rejects skipped resolved styles, mismatched resolved image refs, unsupported work color/dtype and explicit mask geometry/transform mismatch. Mandatory Render admission includes full native detail peak plus retained pinned RGB/alpha, combined protection and all declared mask mappings. Exact current RGB/float32 alpha must match pinned WorkingImage bytes tile by tile before any detail adjustment; uint16 alpha requires upstream qualified normalization with an updated image pin. Existing generated lineage and alpha owners are preserved.

The context loads only Params-declared region/protection arrays through TaskArtifacts and retains their readonly mapping owners through prepare_detail yield. It returns RenderDetailStage(pixels, regions, review_required), supported by base/chain/combined outcome validation. Inherited needs_review and skipped non-zero-strength region outcomes force final publication review; malformed/duplicate/unbounded region identities or inconsistent applied/reason evidence fail validation. Disabled detail still proves current-source binding before yielding unchanged pixels. Qualified mask/model provenance, actual authored module ordering and default factory installation remain installer requirements; this stage does not infer adopted mask authority or publish intermediate images.


#### Portrait detail layer callback

`PinnedDetailLayerProposal` binds LoadedRenderLayer portrait.detail@1, exact runtime SHA, canonical PortraitParams and task-local sealed budgets to prepare_pinned_detail. Its yielded RenderLayerProposal includes RenderLayerEffect.regions and no defect candidates. Shared layer outcome validation permits portrait.detail and defects.repair only, forbids mixed region/candidate evidence, validates every typed region and retains inherited/skipped-region review. Active portrait.detail layers require typed outcomes before blending, just as defects.repair; callback layer/runtime identity substitution fails against composition's retained metadata snapshot. Pure region evidence survives proposal-owner exit and is combined with base effects for final report review. This callback can be installed through RenderLayerProviders alongside its exact PortraitParams operator; full default renderer and authored extension ordering still require explicit installation and source-bound intermediate Params.


#### Layer Params validator ownership

load_render_layer_params snapshots FrozenRenderInputs before runtime callbacks or Params IO. Each validate_binding receives independent copies of typed Params and input/source metadata. The callback must validate only: canonical Params bytes after the callback must still equal the downloaded immutable document, otherwise RENDER_PARAMS_BINDING_CHANGED. Cached Params remain the original validated document; per-layer results own separate metadata. Runtime callback mutation of caller-owned layer selection cannot add/remove/change the loader's retained selection. This ownership boundary does not replace source/model/review authorization, SQL closure or installed runtime qualification, and does not supply missing native binding validators.


#### Shared native denoise preparation

`operations.image.prepare_denoise.prepare_denoise(source, params, engine, scratch, check_active=..., progress=...)` owns canonically validated DenoiseParams and requires finite unit float32 RGB. It retains the existing authored tile/64px halo geometry, work_to_model conversion, strict same-shape finite/unit model output, transfer_model_delta and core-only evidence counts. It yields PreparedDenoise with scoped RGB NPY path/reservation and source_clip/gamut_excess/disabled counts; the engine/model owner must remain live outside this context, and consumers must map or stage before exit. Scratch and mmap owners close on failure/cancellation; Params mutations through model callbacks cannot change retained strength/tile settings. Alpha is preserved by the consumer unchanged. Standalone image.denoise now calls this context and retains existing stage/report/progress behavior.

Render runtime inventory includes this preparation, model_delta and restoration contracts. This extraction does not qualify SCUNet/model bytes, admit native model VRAM/RAM, bind current pixels to source or activate restoration in the full authored renderer. Qualified ModelPool owner, sealed provider peak and exact source/effect evidence remain mandatory for Render installation.


#### Admitted pinned Render denoise stage

`prepare_pinned_denoise(artifacts, params_ref, current, pool, backend=..., execution_profile=..., provider_peak=..., verify_runtime=...)` snapshots DenoiseParams refs/profile/model resource costs, verifies runtime before input IO, requires backend/profile equality and exact pool lock ID, and loads canonical Params/WorkingImage. It admits original pinned RGB/float32 alpha, output owners and halo native buffers alongside the explicit qualified model peak before pixel access. Current RGB/alpha must match the pinned image exactly; mismatches fail before acquiring/invoking the model. No fallback backend/model or invented model costs is allowed.

The qualified pool engine remains acquired through native prepare_denoise, readonly output mapping and RenderDenoiseStage yield; mapping closes before its scratch reservation. Alpha and generated lineage are retained unchanged. Stage includes typed DenoiseReport with actual core-only counts/fractions. Shared effect validation revalidates the report, requires exact warning correspondence to measured clipping/excess, and forces review for gamut warnings or disabled deltas even when a provider declares pass. Full authored restoration ordering, default installation and physical model qualification remain required; this helper does not publish/adopt or accept an unrelated pre-color source.


#### Denoise layer callback and outcome ownership

`PinnedDenoiseLayerProposal` binds image.denoise@1 LoadedRenderLayer Params/runtime to the admitted pinned context with explicit pool/backend/model peak/verification. It returns RenderLayerProposal carrying RenderLayerEffect.denoise_report; candidate/region fields must be empty. Shared validators accept this exact operation and report type, revalidate measured gamut warnings and require review for disabled delta. Non-denoise outcomes must omit denoise_report. Active image.denoise requires typed outcomes before blending. Installed RenderLayerProviders verifies the same operator runtime before dispatch; pinned callback independently verifies native/model qualification through its configured verifier. Neither check permits automatic qualification or fallback.

Composition owns a deep copy of every typed layer outcome before provider scope exit. Pydantic region/report objects can otherwise be mutated by provider teardown despite frozen outer dataclasses. Final review validation uses the retained copy and never expired proposal pixel maps. Full authored restoration ordering/default installation and real hardware qualification remain incomplete.


#### Shared scoped native upscale

`operations.image.prepare_upscale.prepare_upscale(source, alpha, params, engine, scratch, check_active=..., progress=...)` owns canonically validated UpscaleParams, validates finite unit float32 RGB and the 200M output pixel limit, and accepts explicit uint16 or finite unit float32 alpha. It retains existing 256px/32px halo tiles, resize_work_linear base, cubic model baseline, strict scaled model output and gamut-safe transfer_model_delta with core-only evidence counts. Alpha retains its input precision and existing linear-resampling semantics. Writer mappings close before yielding PreparedUpscale paths/reservation/counts, and cleanup occurs on model error/cancellation/context exit. Consumers must stage/map within scope and hold a qualified acquired model outside it.

Standalone image.upscale uses this context with its existing uint16 alpha path and unchanged canvas/half-pixel ResizeTransform/report publication. Render runtime includes new native preparation bytes. This helper does not transform layer masks/protection/generated lineage, enforce installed model budgets or connect upscale to authored Render. A geometry-aware pipeline must bind the new canvas/transform and preserve lineage before enabling restoration upscale; it must not treat this as a same-canvas proposal. Full default installation and physical model qualification remain required.


#### Shared upscale geometry and same-canvas exclusion

`upscale_geometry(image, params)` owns/revalidates WorkingImage/UpscaleParams and returns the existing source-identity-preserving up2/up4 canvas plus a half-pixel invertible ResizeTransform appended to the prior TransformChain. The valid rectangle spans the exact scaled output and the 200M output limit is mandatory. Standalone upscale uses this shared metadata builder. Render preparation and same-canvas adjustment composition explicitly reject active image.upscale before pixel/mask/proposal IO with RENDER_GEOMETRY_STAGE_REQUIRED. This is an explicit guard until the geometry-aware output pipeline is implemented; it must not prevent a future correct post-color upscale stage or force upscale into optical pre-color ordering. New masks/protection/generated lineage and final independent adoption remain required before enabling upscale in full Render.


#### Scoped resize mask assembly

`prepare_resize_masks(entries, generated, step, source_chain, guide, scratch, ...)` validates a ResizeTransform, owned source TransformChain and at most 257 unique (MaskRef, pixels) entries. Every explicit mask must match from_canvas/source_chain before owner allocation. It invokes existing prepare_transform probability resampling and complete guided-refinement halo using the target work-RGB guide; yielded masks retain source identity, readonly target pixels and measured guided radius. Source MaskRef in each tuple is provenance only, not a target published MaskRef. The publisher must register new target bytes and construct target metadata with to_canvas and the returned appended chain.

Generated lineage uses existing bilinear transform_tiles geometry without guided inference/content refinement and yields a readonly scoped map. All transformed array/PNG/reservation owners remain alive through yield and close on cancellation/exit. Caller must admit RAM/scratch peaks, hold source/guide owners and prove source/model/mask authority before calling. This helper does not restore protected RGB, publish masks or enable full Render upscale; geometry-aware original restoration and final adoption remain required.


#### Shared transformed mask publication

`operations.mask.transform_publish.stage_transform` owns/revalidates MaskTransformParams/target WorkingImage, proves target canvas/chain and label/probability radius consistency before staging scoped array/PNG with existing output roles. It creates target MaskRef preserving source object_id/selector/confidence/model_lock_id/protected and returns a typed TransformedMask plus two pixel outputs/counts. `finish_transform` stages the result manifest; standalone mask.transform calls it after scratch/input owners close, preserving its prior lifecycle. `publish_transform` composes both for a caller retaining a broader live render scope. These functions stage artifacts only, not commit/adopt them.

`GeometryMasks.files` retains scoped transform file owners alongside readonly target arrays. `publish_resize_masks` owns target metadata/ref, prevalidates the entire explicit mask inventory against target canvas/chain before first stage, and publishes each result through the shared helper. It must execute within prepare_resize_masks scope. Generated lineage remains a separate owner requiring its dedicated publication/lineage contract; it is not mislabeled as an explicit analysis mask. #### Mandatory resize mask resource admission

prepare_resize_masks now requires keyword-only execution_profile and snapshots it before callbacks. `resize_masks_peak(step, count, has_generated=..., tile=..., max_work_pixels=...)` counts all concurrently retained source masks, raw/refined NPY owners, PNG hard bounds, target guide, generated source/output and one complete guided-filter working halo. Count/type/tile/200M limits and guided working-pixel budget are checked before allocation. Mandatory admit_render targets the resized canvas pixel_count and adds ordinary retained Render costs; insufficient RAM/scratch/output budgets fail before prepare_transform or generated reservation. Installers must supply a sealed geometry-stage profile covering the target canvas and all additional retained model/source resources. These conservative static estimates do not establish physical qualification or complete geometry pipeline activation.


#### Resized generated coverage publication

GeometryMasks retains generated_path/generated_owner only while its context is live. `publish_resize_generated(artifacts, prepared, target, step, ...)` owns target WorkingImage, requires the exact target canvas/appended chain/final ResizeTransform, validates scoped generated layout/owner and stages pixels under existing mask_pixels/application/x-npy followed by a MaskRef contract under mask. The new mask uses target geometry, selector=generated, confidence=1, protected=false and no model_lock_id, matching existing Render staging semantics. It returns PublishedGeometryLineage with mask/ref/outputs/byte count; absent lineage returns None without staging. Owner/file/geometry checks precede the first stage.

This publishes coverage only. Original generation/inpaint origin reports and dependency closure must still accompany the full result; a resized generated mask does not prove model identity, authorize generation or replace report lineage. Geometry-aware RGB/protection restoration, final result assembly, fenced commit and independent adoption remain required before enabling full Render upscale.


#### Upscale protected baseline preservation

prepare_upscale optionally accepts read_protected(x, y, width, height) in the resized target canvas. Every supplied tile must be finite unit float32 with exact core dimensions. All positive protected pixels are restored bit-exactly from the existing resize_work_linear halo/core baseline before writing model-enhanced output. Alpha already follows baseline linear resampling and is unaffected by RGB model delta. Without read_protected, existing standalone results remain unchanged. Source must have undergone qualified original RGB/alpha restoration before upscale; transformed protection and source/guide owners remain caller responsibilities. This does not substitute pre-geometry pixel coordinates for target coordinates or use an unrelated baseline.

Native gamut counts continue to describe the model candidate core; final protected result still requires independent quality measurement. preserve_render_protection now retains AdjustmentPixels.effects when returning restored pixels, so final source restoration cannot drop prior layer review metadata. Full geometry-aware Render assembly/adoption is still required.


#### Retained upscale baseline for transformed protection

prepare_upscale accepts strict boolean retain_baseline (default false). When true, its reservation includes another 12 bytes per target pixel and it writes the exact resize_work_linear core baseline during the same model pass. PreparedUpscale.baseline_path is scoped to the native reservation; all writer mappings close before yield. This path permits target-guided prepare_resize_masks to finish before preserve_render_protection restores positive transformed protection from the baseline. No second model pass, fabricated inference or image-dependent substitution of the baseline is necessary. Alpha already uses the original linear-resampling baseline; generated lineage remains the explicitly transformed map.

Caller must admit the retained baseline alongside native model, transformed mask/PNG and restored output owners, and prove source protection was applied upstream. Candidate gamut counts are retained separately from independent final quality measurement. Default standalone mode retains neither baseline nor new cost. Full authored geometry installer/closure/publication/adoption remains incomplete.


#### Geometry-aware native upscale assembly

`prepare_upscale_assembly(artifacts, image_ref, image, current, params, engine, entries, execution_profile=..., provider_peak=..., verify_runtime=...)` owns/revalidates source image/Params/explicit mask metadata and preserves prior layer outcomes. Params.image must equal image_ref; source work color/dtype, RGB layout, alpha presence/precision and explicit mask canvas/chain must match. The qualified installer must additionally prove current source bytes, adopted model/mask provenance and pool owner; the context does not manufacture those authorities.

It verifies configured runtime and admits the complete target-canvas pipeline before native outputs: explicit model peak plus source owners, baseline/candidate/restored target owners, halo buffers and all retained mask/refined/PNG/generated owners. It runs prepare_upscale exactly once with baseline retention, maps readonly candidate/baseline/float32 alpha, performs target-guided resize masks, combines every protected target tile by maximum and restores all positive protected RGB from baseline using preserve_render_protection. Alpha follows baseline resampling; generated lineage and prior effect metadata survive. RenderUpscaleAssembly exposes scoped pixels, new canvas/chain, live mask/publication owners and native measured candidate counts. Consumers must stage outputs before scope exit and preserve origin report closure.

The profile must admit resized pixel_count; graph scheduler/default installer must plan this explicitly. This assembly does not install an operation, publish a root or replace final independent quality/SQL/fenced adoption. Unsupported same-canvas preparation remains denied until full geometry-aware authored execution is connected.


#### Upscale intermediate image/report publication

prepare_upscale_assembly now requires explicit backend matching the sealed execution profile and retains owned source WorkingImage plus measured UpscaleReport in RenderUpscaleAssembly. The report binds image/model lock/backend/scale/tile/overlap and actual native candidate counts, never substitutes final independent quality metrics.

`stage_upscale_assembly(artifacts, prepared, ...)` runs inside the assembly scope. It then publishes every transformed explicit mask bound to the newly staged image ref plus resized generated coverage. Original source/generation reports and independent target quality measurement remain mandatory in the full ImageResult.


#### Upscale staging preflight

RenderUpscaleAssembly retains source_ref, owned UpscaleParams and explicit backend. stage_upscale_assembly derives the exact expected canvas/chain from those Params/source, matches report source/model/backend/scale/tile/overlap and actual native core counts/fractions/warnings, validates RGB/alpha layout and finite unit values, and verifies the complete explicit mask owner geometry/radius/files before first artifact write. Prior effect records are all validated before IO; earlier review cannot short-circuit a later invalid record. A changed report/model pin, altered measured fraction, wrong output geometry or NaN final pixels fails RENDER_UPSCALE_STAGE_INVALID without staging. These in-process guards complement, and do not replace, authoritative adopted source/model/graph identity and independent final quality measurements.


#### Pinned upscale TaskArtifacts/ModelPool context

`prepare_pinned_upscale(artifacts, params_ref, current, pool, mask_refs=..., backend=..., execution_profile=..., provider_peak=..., verify_runtime=...)` owns explicit ref/profile/budget metadata, verifies runtime, loads canonical UpscaleParams/WorkingImage/MaskRef documents, requires exact pool model lock and matching work-color/source mask geometry. Shared upscale_assembly_peak admits complete target geometry costs before pixel downloads/mapping. Exact current RGB/float32 alpha must match pinned source NPY bits tile by tile. Explicit masks are mapped through TaskArtifacts inside one owner stack, with duplicate pixel refs denied. Model lock is rechecked after input callbacks before pool acquisition.

The configured ModelPool engine is acquired through the full prepare_upscale_assembly yield, including target masks/protection/baseline/publication owners. Caller must supply adopted mask/model provenance, generated origin closure, qualified peak and graph-level approved runtime/source binding; declared membership and same-canvas metadata alone are insufficient. Metadata IO remains under upstream task admission. This context cannot approve review, install the default factory or submit final fenced adoption.


#### Upscale borrowed mask pixel preflight

After complete resource admission and before native output allocation/inference, prepare_upscale_assembly checks every explicit source mask and optional generated coverage for exact source layout, float32 dtype and finite unit values using bounded tiles. Invalid shape/dtype/NaN/range fails RENDER_GEOMETRY_MASK_PIXELS_INVALID with no engine invocation or scratch owners. Mask provenance/model/adopted source authority remains independently mandatory; pixel validation cannot establish it. Current service still installs Render through an injected renderer object; full default CLI provider factory and all authored operator wiring remain incomplete.

### Full Render dependency loading

`v2draft.RenderRecipeSource.Load(ctx, actor, tenant, authored_document, authored_bytes, binding, icc_lock, model_lock)` is the server metadata boundary for full Render freezing. The server must obtain authored bytes/document/binding from the authorized current draft, and ICC/model locks from the trusted qualified profile; these are not client-supplied frozen values. The loader owns authored bytes before dependency callbacks, traverses the scoped recipe closure, reads exact source/style/correction/override/layer object versions, checks source canvas and ICC, and preserves every ordered complete OverrideV1 document. A scoped SQL catalog must match the requested project and asset version.

`prepare_render_style` snapshots inputs and caller scalar evidence before object callbacks and independently rejects nonzero OverrideV1 scalar contributions with `RENDER_OVERRIDE_SCALAR_DOUBLE_APPLICATION`, even if a submitted frozen total includes the same vector.

For this full authored Render path, OverrideV1 module fields are absolute whole-module replacements. Their `ResolvedCorrection.Vector` contribution is zero: applying a delta derived from their absolute gains/smoothing after module replacement would apply the same edit twice. Session correction, pinned learning prediction and recipe.parameter_delta remain additive. Parameter-only training extraction is a separate path and retains its existing conversion of accepted absolute scalar targets into differences against the common base; it must not supply those training vectors as full Render module evidence.

When learning is referenced, `RenderLearningReader` must prove active eligible version/consent and adopted frame provenance. The concrete Service implementation performs those SQL reads. The loader rejects ICC/model lock disagreement, changed source binding, absent face/shard pins or invalid descriptor ordinal and computes prediction from the pinned profile/features. A detached session correction is passed to the descriptor reader to prevent mutation of the frozen scalar evidence. `LoadedRenderRecipe` owns authored/style/ordered override/layer bytes and returns the encoded source SHA required for background selection. It is backend metadata, not a public input format. Worker full StyleV2/merged bounds validation, actual qualification, current-draft CAS, freeze endpoint wiring, immutable publication and task admission remain separate gates.

### Server final scalar bounds after full module replacement

`BuildFrozenRenderRecipeFromDocuments(authored, resolved_inputs, layer_body, accepted_style_body, ordered_override_bodies, encoded_source_sha)` is the document-backed full Render compiler. It proves canonical exact style/override pins, authored ref order and source-bound OverrideV1 metadata; requires zero additive override vectors; replaces complete global/beauty/extensions modules in authored order and extracts the resulting scalar baseline. Missing/null gains, saturation, beauty enabled flag or enabled smoothing are invalid, not implicit zero values. All gains, saturation and smoothing remain in existing StyleV2 bounds. The complete final additive delta is then validated against this merged baseline through the existing CheckStyleBounds formula, preserving the original base_style reference and authored bytes in the frozen wire.

The source loader and selected-background publication path use this merged-baseline validation. Background selection applies the same final scalar check before catalog lookup. Thus a base gain4 replaced by gain1 accepts a small positive EV, while gain1 replaced by gain4 rejects that EV; neither uses hidden clamping. Standalone/scalar training ResolveRecipe keeps its existing common-base semantics. Low-level BuildFrozenRenderRecipe still requires a trusted correctly resolved baseline; callers with complete module documents must use the document-backed compiler or selected publication path. This scalar/module identity validation does not validate every non-scalar StyleV2 field or model dependency: complete worker schema validation and qualified provider coverage remain mandatory. The freeze endpoint and atomic current-draft revision fence are still separate required work.

`Publisher.PublishFrozenRenderDraftRecipe` validates an owned FrozenRenderRecipe, uploads through the existing immutable protocol, and calls `RegisterFrozenRenderDraftRecipe` with a distinct authored draft document and expected revision. It must never first call the unfenced registration method. `CompileAndPublishSelectedFrozenRenderDraftRecipe` owns authored/layer/scalar evidence, validates merged document-backed bounds and layers/learning, resolves any selected imported background from server authority and uses this fenced publication path. The final SQL transaction independently proves exact current draft revision and authored SHA. Current-draft loading, trusted qualification/model lock selection, public freeze request identity and endpoint routing remain required service work.


#### Render freeze pending execution snapshot

SQLRenderFreezeService first performs readFreezeReplay with fresh tenant/editor/project authorization. A completed record revalidates frozen upload scope and returns canonical public result. A live pending record with a nonempty body must pass decodeRenderFreezePending: strict exact envelope {schema_version:1,document_id,draft_document_id,draft_revision,recipe}, deterministic request bindings, canonical JSON, envelope≤1049600 bytes, recipe≤1048576 bytes and DecodeFrozenRenderRecipe semantic validation. Unknown, aliased, missing or mismatched fields fail503; expired pending records fail410; changed request hash fails409.

If no snapshot exists, full document compilation happens outside SQL. pinFreezePending reauthorizes asset/project, inserts the existing24h idempotency row if missing, locks it FOR UPDATE before any domain locks, checks request hash/expiry, and either preserves the first existing valid snapshot or stores the newly compiled canonical recipe. A concurrently completed command is re-read through the normal completed-scope path. Only the final registration transaction sets response_status200 and replaces private snapshot JSON with RenderFreezeResult. Recovery derives the original version/source binding from FrozenRenderRecipe.Scalars.Source and calls Publisher.Publish with the stable document ID and original body. Ready uploads need no object writes; uncompleted immutable uploads use existing PutImmutable/RecoverImmutable. register_v26_render_draft_recipe still performs the final current-draft revision/hash and live source/background checks atomically with public reply storage. No new table, migration, broad role privilege or production policy activation is introduced.


After register_v26_render_draft_recipe succeeds inside idempotency.Use, validateFrozenLearningScope rechecks any frozen learning evidence in the same transaction using v26_render_model_scope and v26_render_descriptor_scope. Model scope proves the original authored model-use link, current active/eligible version, live training consent and ready exact model/training artifacts. Descriptor scope proves exact analysis/model-lock/source/ordinal, adopted session.describe result, producer attempt/fence and scene membership. False returns SNAPSHOT_EXPIRED410; the surrounding transaction rolls back registration and public response together, retaining pending evidence. No new model prediction or recipe mutation is performed. These existing scope functions are live read predicates; this guard alone does not prove serializable exclusion against simultaneous consent revocation. Migration00465 persists checked learning/model-lock evidence in private upload metadata; completed HTTP replay revalidates that evidence through frozen draft scope. Downstream render admission/adoption also retains its model/consent checks. Dedicated learning-enabled fresh freeze/recovery and completed-replay revocation acceptance remain required.


After migration00465 the final service calls register_v26_render_draft_recipe_result: registered proceeds to the existing Go guard and public response, conflict maps409, learning_expired maps410, unknown outcomes fail503. The compatible boolean SQL function remains available for existing callers; its false result leaves no partial core registration. The immutable object and pending idempotency evidence remain available for normal cleanup or authorized retry; no revoked learning pin is replaced with a new prediction under the original key.


### V27 CPU assembly: transport scratch accounting

`CompileSemanticAssemblyProfile` freezes CPU/v2.2 resource estimates of256MiB RAM,32MiB scratch,0 VRAM and8MiB output. Scratch includes the mandatory retained V2InputIndex reservation of at least16MiB, up to8MiB plan staging and8MiB margin. `execute_assembly` rejects smaller admitted scratch before document reads or staging. Scheduler orchestrator/store and forward-only00530 stage gate use the same32MiB profile; neither index reserve nor output limits are weakened. ### V27 native defect result transaction boundary

`ReadNativeDefectResultEvidence` retains a private `nativeDefectAdmissionProof` only after verifying native canonical output identity, original Params/candidates/report links, physical NPY/PNG/protection, selected landmark and mask roots. The proof owns exact original five-object metadata, PNG metadata, ordered native catalog refs and canonical result UUID. Original and PNG metadata are rechecked after object IO before the final canonical catalog recheck; every error returns an empty result.

`SQLTransferSourceReader.lockNativeDefectResult(ctx,tx,who,tenant,binding,proof)` is a DB-only component for a caller-owned scheduler transaction. It validates private proof scope and cross-links before SQL, requires `photo_scheduler`, sets transaction-local tenant context and invokes scheduler-only `photo.lock_semantic_defect_native_result` (00726). SQL composes live actor/session/membership/project/accepted source authority, the tenant inventory writer barrier, original producer/task attempt/result/object locks, output-upload locks and exact PNG metadata, then resolves current native catalog again. Canonical result UUID, all five ordered refs, Params and physical object metadata must equal retained values. A failure requires rollback of the entire caller transaction. No object-store operation occurs in this lock.

This boundary does not certify model qualification or all input producer policies, bind an arbitrary result to a parent request, approve candidates, admit review, reserve parent capacity, seal a parent graph, or emit a parent continuation event. Those remain mandatory components of the complete continuation transaction. The native preparation configuration already records parent run ID, request SHA, intent and snapshot; continuation must explicitly bind these to the owned parent request and retain that binding under the result/run locks. Physical verification process 62113 proves the lock and changed-result-ID refusal for one real CPU/mTLS fixture, plus the existing two-tenant checks; it does not prove the full continuation path.


### V27 native result parent request binding

`ReadNativeDefectResultForRequest(ctx,who,request,key,intentRef,snapshotRef,taskID)` recompiles the canonical owned request and deterministic parent/workflow identity from the key. It requires the authenticated actor, original request SHA/body/intent equality and pinned canonical intent SHA. Successful reads retain private `nativeDefectParentProof`. `lockNativeDefectContinuation` first composes 00726 canonical output/scope locks, then scheduler-only `lock_semantic_defect_native_parent` rechecks association while holding native task/run locks. This proves a specific parent request's native result association; it does not verify parent graph admission, authorize review, qualify models, produce a queued parent ticket, or emit continuation events. The current native worker returns raw `defect_protection` NPY plus report linkage. Transfer consumes a separate `MaskRef` document with original-source geometry and registered canonical provenance. Merely replacing SourceProtection with the raw NPY is invalid. `BuildSemanticDefectProtectionDocument(ctx,paramsRef,paramsBody,reportRef,reportBody,proposalRef,proposalBody,binding,imageBody)` provides the content conversion for that derived root. `prepareNativeDefectProtection` owns publication input as a private document UUID, immutable canonical body string, original protection pixels ref and private parent/result proof. It performs exact-parent full content reads, reads the original source, builds the report's inline MaskRef and repeats full native parent/content verification afterward; mismatched retained proof returns no preparation. Document ID is UUIDv5(parent run, "semantic.defect.protection:" + native task UUID).

`v2draft.Publisher.PublishSemanticProtectionDocument` validates source-bound protected MaskRef geometry and digest, then uses the existing accepted-version document reservation/quota/fencing and immutable publication/recovery path. A repeated identical request returns the same artifact ref. Publication alone does not register a canonical protection root or grant model/parent policy authority. ### V27 registered native protection provenance and consumption

`semantic_native_protections` (00728) is an insert-only tenant-scoped registry. It records document/project/accepted version/source pins, owned parent run, native task/canonical result, published root ref, original protection NPY ref, original report ref and exact saved anchor. `semantic_native_protection_objects` (00729, API-only) returns exactly the registered root and original NPY catalog entries for a live exact-parent/native canonical result. `ReadRegisteredNativeDefectProtection` verifies full native documents/provenance/NPY, reads the pinned published MaskRef, compares its complete bytes with the original report-inline document, retains exact metadata and repeats parent/native/root catalog checks after object IO. `PublishRegisteredNativeDefectProtection` owns the compiled request and composes preparation, immutable publication, SQL registration and final verified read. It proves content and provenance only; it does not grant candidate approval, full model policy authority or a queued parent ticket.

`VerifyNativeSelectionSeparation` streams the source-bound selected mask and original protection NPY through existing `VerifyMaskSeparation`, requiring a nonempty selection and no positive protected overlap. The physical fixture uses an independent NumPy oracle reading exact SHA/byte-pinned object versions; overlapping valid masks require zero-count ErrInvalid, disjoint valid masks require the exact independently counted selected support. That conditional oracle must not be replaced by an assumption that every authored selection passes after new protection is generated.

`VerifyNativePersonProtectionCoverage` independently discovers a single mask.person candidate from operator expectations, retains its original root/body and Params/Report evidence before qualification IO, verifies the original qualification report and actual producer profile, then streams coverage against registered native protection. Any positive person probability requires protection. Candidate, evidence, qualification, arrays and registered native catalog are rechecked after IO.
