## Native target prerequisite manifest (implementation foundation)

compileNativeTargetPreparationManifest(ctx,who,request,key,published,snapshot,body)
recompiles the original request and validates the exact pinned snapshot against
the entire published target inventory. Private canonical schema1 retains tenant,
project, actor, session, original parent/request SHA, intent/snapshot refs and
entries in ordinal order. Each entry retains exact binding, namespace,
reuse_reference and stages. Namespace is UUIDv5(original parent,
"semantic.target.prepare:1:"+snapshotSHA+":"+ordinal+":"+JCSBindingSHA).
Stage IDs are UUIDv5(namespace,"stage:"+operation), not executable task IDs.
Each stage fixes operation_version1. Person and detector have no prerequisites;
landmarks/parser depend on detector, skin on parser, defects on person+landmarks+skin.
Exact reference-binding entries have no target stages: reuse_reference is a hint
requiring the original verified protection/person authority, not permission to
reuse arbitrary source-wide output. Maximum5000 entries and canonical8MiB.
Cancellation/error returns empty manifest.

verifyNativeTargetPreparationManifest recompiles original scope and compares
exact canonical bytes/SHA. Internal nativeTargetPreparationRequired retains this
owned manifest while unwrapping the existing dependency-unavailable error.
admitInitialPersonDefectParent creates it only on missing target protection;
ambiguous or failed evidence is not redirected into generation. No public
receipt, task, Params or output reference is synthesized by this step.

This is the planning foundation. Persistence, original-snapshot transactional
authority, per-target compiler support, qualified budgets, production scheduling,
canonical output/adoption and lifecycle/concurrency/DB/S3 tests remain required.
It must not be marked as complete target-producer orchestration.


### V27: восстановление сохранённого target manifest — граница доверия

`restoreNativeTargetPreparationManifest` принимает исходные `Identity`, `Request`, idempotency key, `PublishedIntent`, pinned snapshot ref/body и недоверенные manifest bytes/SHA. До восстановления: cancellation check, размер 2..8MiB, SHA lowercase hex64 и SHA256 исходных bytes. Затем полная повторная компиляция из исходных входов и побайтовое сравнение с canonical manifest. JSON с дополнительными полями, whitespace, заменёнными scope/namespace/dependencies/version/reuse или усечённым inventory не принимается, даже если SHA пересчитан. Успех возвращает собственную копию bytes; ошибка возвращает пустое значение. Это private codec: SQL authorization, фиксация в БД, qualification, quota admission и task scheduling обязательны отдельно. Наличие codec не закрывает durable recovery и весь V27.


### V27: durable target checkpoint / 00912

`TransferDraftPreparer.checkpointNativeTargetPreparation` проверяет исходный detector seal и recompilation manifest до TX; берёт scheduler transaction, устанавливает tenant context и вызывает `save_v27_target_manifest` с original config/intent/snapshot. SQL возвращает оригинальные сохранённые bytes и SHA. Restore codec повторно сверяет их с original request/key/project/session/inventory; только после этого commit. Ошибка возвращает пустой план, rollback; object IO в TX отсутствует. Caller держит исходный parent advisory lock.

`SQLNativeInitialPipelineRecovery.Recover`: после original person/face/defect success и отсутствия target protection private `nativeTargetPreparationRequired` приводит к этому checkpoint. Успех возвращает `waiting` для `semantic.transfer` с пустым producer outcome. Он не подтверждает admission/completed и сохраняет recovery активным; последующий проход обязан получить тот же original plan. SQL handoff gates00911 сохранены. Target executors/qualification/quota admission пока не реализованы: durable checkpoint не разрешает scheduled stage execution и не закрывает V27.


### V27: target-bound person draft

`nativeTargetAnchor` содержит owned original Request/key, PublishedIntent/full inventory, pinned snapshot ref/body, immutable manifest/body/SHA, выбранные entry/stage и private fingerprint. `compileNativeTargetAnchor` восстанавливает manifest через full recompilation, проверяет ordinal и operation, отвергает reference reuse; исходный EditIntent.Reference не меняется.

`prepareNativeTargetPersonPlan` выбирает текущую person policy, публикует Params для target binding в document UUIDv5(target namespace,"semantic.mask.person.params:reference"), вызывает lower source/person preparation с parent=target namespace и читает физические target RGB/alpha/geometry/ICC и qualification report. Policy повторяется после IO. `compileNativeTargetPersonPlan` повторно строит anchor, требует exact source scope, binding и namespace, Params document identity, frozen task identity и catalog input pins, report/task SHA, точные model/runtime/device profile SHA, output+8MiB reservation. Ordinary-parent continuation запрещён. Run UUIDv5(target namespace,"semantic.mask.person:reference"); workflow=WorkflowID(run), task=TaskID(workflow,"mask.person",0). Manifest stage ID — отдельный prerequisite ID, не task ID.

Config `v27_target_person_prepare` фиксирует schema_version1, original parent_run_id/request_sha/auth_session_id/intent/snapshot, target_ordinal/namespace/stage_id, manifest_sha, target source binding, params/document/task/input_digest/policy_sha/recovery. `nativeTargetPersonPlan` хранит anchor, task/source/person proof, shell и fingerprint; изменение fingerprints не заменяет SQL authority.

`beginNativeTargetPersonInTransaction` повторно компилирует plan, сравнивает fingerprint и deployed ICC, вызывает `lockNativeTargetAnchorInTransaction` на exact original detector (SQL00913), затем существующие source/person qualification/Params/policy locks для target binding. Generic BeginAdmission сохраняет только blocked draft + reservation + pending idempotency record key `target-person-prep:<run>`. Только created/pending с exact run допустимы. Caller держит original parent advisory lock и rollback любого отказа. Target transport registration, dedicated seal, worker canonical adoption/result authority и recovery ещё не реализованы. Не вызывать reference `compilePersonDraftPlan`/`sealPersonPreparation` с target plan; подстановка original reference/RequestSHA запрещена.


### V27 target person transport contract (00914–00915)

`publishNativeTargetPersonTransport` verifies the owned target plan, exact frozen task and qualification binding before object IO, publishes the immutable input closure outside transactions, then verifies the plan again and freezes registration evidence. Cancellation/failure yields no accepted transport.

`registerNativeTargetPersonInTransaction` binds published transport to that exact frozen task, repeats target begin/current original-entry authorization and target source/Params/qualification/policy locks, and checks the SQL registration state. Missing registers the entire closure transactionally and must become replayed; replayed is an exact no-op; conflict aborts. Caller owns rollback and original parent advisory lock. Pending admission validates registered profiles through00915, without re-reserving quota. Publication cannot authorize execution.

The run remains draft, graph unsealed, task blocked and canonical result absent. Dedicated target seal, worker admission/execution/adoption, other target stages and automatic recovery orchestration remain required before V27 module acceptance. The physical fixture uses deterministic ML doubles and a distinct asset version sharing immutable WorkingImage bytes; it is transport/scope evidence, not real-GPU image-quality qualification.

It marshals the existing person qualification, Params pin, complete source file inventory, canonical registration binding and source pins into a dedicated original/target envelope. Original request/reference/snapshot text stay unchanged; target binding is represented only in target evidence. Cancellation, invalid transport, scope drift or oversize evidence return no body.

SQL `lock_semantic_target_person_admission_evidence` independently repeats original entry authority, target graph scope/registration closure, config/policy hashes, source and qualification locks and live quota budget. A positive return grants no queue authority. Caller keeps original parent advisory lock and transaction ownership. A future dedicated seal must retain these locks through atomic activation/event/ticket commit and independently validate sealed replay; generic/source native seal cannot be used on target configuration.

`sealNativeTargetPersonInTransaction` compiles the dedicated original/target evidence and calls the fixed target SQL seal query through the existing scheduler-only receipt decoder. Exact sealed replay uses separate private validators and repeats live authorization; a previously saved ticket is never sufficient by itself. Target/reference compilers and original intent stay distinct.

Physical seal fixture adds revocation before first seal, successful seal, exact replay, revocation after seal and restored authorized replay. `beginNativeTargetSkinDraftInTransaction` requires caller-owned original parent advisory lock and scheduler transaction; caller rolls back every error. It verifies/recompiles the owned target skin plan, compiles the actual mask.face_skin target anchor from original request/manifest/ordinal and locks current original/target authority. It requires a completed, sealed, non-cancelled target parser run in addition to a succeeded canonical task. A running parser must refuse without persisting a child. It checks color identity and locks the complete canonical physical target-parser result through dedicated target face consumer evidence, binding exact parser and detector transports. It validates immutable namespace Params pin/byte count and locks current complete qualification policy before generic blocked BeginAdmission. Only exact created/pending outcomes with deterministic target run ID are accepted. No object IO, registration, seal or queue activation occurs in the transaction.

Physical acceptance requires exact pending retry, revoked-session refusal, restored-authority retry and persisted draft/unsealed/blocked state with no queued event. Independent SQL/S3 verification must confirm namespace run/workflow/task/stage/Params IDs, completed canonical target-parser dependency, source binding and actual immutable Params bytes/checksum. Registered replay, admission/seal, worker result/finalization and saved automatic recovery remain mandatory. Migration 00940 adds only the narrow scheduler-only completed parser barrier; generic registered pending matcher must still be extended before target skin registration. Direct scheduler table access is prohibited.

### V27 target skin registration / 00941

`marshalNativeTargetSkinRegistrationBinding` verifies owned target plan and frozen CPU task transport, producing the strict bounded registration packet with exact Params/profile/input digest and full transport proof. `registerNativeTargetSkinInputsInTransaction` repeats dedicated target blocked begin with original parent lock and exact parser/detector transports, then queries dedicated binding state. Missing permits canonical registration exactly once; replay returns after repeated current authority; conflict/partial state refuses. Post-registration must be replayed. Object publication occurs outside the transaction through scoped target-run publisher; no transaction spans object IO. Admission/seal, worker output/canonical result/finalization and saved automatic recovery remain required.

### V27 target skin admission / 00942

`marshalNativeTargetSkinDependencyEvidence` verifies the owned target skin plan, complete target parser private result fingerprint and plan identity, hashes retained manifest/Params bodies and emits exact parser output/Params object pins plus full target detector result pins. `marshalNativeTargetSkinAdmissionEvidence` recompiles the dedicated target skin plan and combines complete target parser admission evidence, physical dependency packet, immutable config text, bounded Params pin and exact skin registration. The shared `nativeSkinAdmissionEvidence` type defines serialization only; source/target authority compilers remain separate.

`lockNativeTargetSkinAdmissionInTransaction` requires scheduler role, sets local tenant context and invokes the fixed target-only SQL barrier. Caller owns original parent advisory lock/rollback; no object IO occurs in the transaction. It does not seal or activate. Null/substituted required sections and revoked sessions must fail. Seal, worker claim/output/canonical result, finish and saved automatic advancement remain required.

### V27 target skin atomic seal / 00943

`sealNativeTargetSkinInTransaction` compiles full dedicated target skin admission packet and uses the fixed target-only seal query through the bounded common ticket decoder. Caller owns original parent lock, transaction commit and rollback. Object publication precedes the transaction; no object IO occurs during seal. First activation requires live admission and exact registered CPU task. Queued receipt replay repeats current authority and refuses revoked sessions. Retry must preserve exactly one queue event/outbox item. This does not complete the prerequisite or original parent. Worker claim/output, physical result/finalization and saved automatic recovery remain mandatory.

### V27 target skin worker execution

Target skin uses the existing CPU adapter and indexed mTLS/S3 transport. 00944 makes qualified claim and run-start atomic under exact target scope. 00945 guards canonical commit by exact native output ownership/order. Physical worker test checks float32 binary mask, exact uint16 PNG correspondence and MaskRef lineage, negative attempt/fence/hash/foreign/duplicate/reordered proofs, canonical commit replay and post-task-success sealed receipt replay. Prerequisite run remains running until dedicated physical result validation/finalization. Upstream ML fixtures are deterministic doubles; these tests prove protocol/content invariants, not real-photo skin quality. Result/finish and saved automatic recovery remain required.

`nativeTargetSkinResultCatalog` and `nativeTargetSkinResultObjects` use dedicated API queries with bounded decoding, exact forbidden input refs and ordered four-object pins. `readCompletedNativeTargetSkin` reads exact canonical target parser proof and skin output/Params bytes, validates MaskRef/WorkingImage/source binding, and uses existing verifyNativeSkinDerivation to prove float32 mask and uint16 PNG agree with the complete parser label stream. It repeats catalog, objects, parser proof and label pixel pins after IO. Source readers are not used with substituted target references.

Shared completed evidence types define serialization only. `lockNativeCompletedTargetSkinInTransaction` recompiles owned target plan, requires scheduler role/local tenant, and calls the dedicated fixed SQL consumer barrier. finishNativeTargetSkinInTransaction first repeats dedicated completed skin consumer lock, then calls the fixed target-only finalizer with the same physical evidence packet. Completed physical read, packet generation, consumer and sealed receipt replay must remain valid; worker output predicate must refuse completed producer. ### V27 target skin saved restoration / 00949

readNativeTargetSkinSaved validates actual target skin anchor and deterministic saved run/task/config identity and supported phase. restoreNativeTargetSkinPlan reads exact immutable namespace Params and current complete physical target parser result, restores saved CPU RAM/scratch/output/reserved limits and SkinProfileVersion, recompiles task/plan and compares saved config SHA/input digest/Params/task/profile. Skin recovery must contain no model qualification report, original request locator or ordinary continuation metadata. It performs no object IO/repair/queueing. Queued/completed restore must reproduce original plan fingerprint and sealed receipt; config/profile/Params substitutions refuse. ### V27 target skin automatic advancement

`advanceNativeTargetSkins` runs under the original parent lock and restores the immutable target manifest. For every non-reference entry it restores completed target detector and parser plans, their exact persisted transports and sealed receipts. Missing skin stages publish namespace Params, compile the CPU task from admitted server limits, persist blocked metadata, publish scoped transport, then register and seal atomically. Existing stages restore saved CPU limits/profile and exact Params without replacing capacity or repairing metadata. Queued stages return waiting; succeeded tasks require repeated physical parser/skin proof and completed evidence before the dedicated finish transaction. Completed retries repeat authority and finalization. The result remains waiting for semantic.transfer; no prerequisite may complete the original parent.

`NativeInitialPipelineRecoveryJob` calls skin advancement only after target person, detector and face advancement report prerequisite readiness. Target defects and original transfer admission remain separate required stages. Automatic helper tests must exercise missing, queued restore, canonical worker commit, finish and completed retry, with independent SQL/S3 verification. A helper PASS is not fresh-process Recover proof or V27 acceptance.

### V27 target defect physical preparation and Params publication

`nativeTargetDefectPreparation` retains dedicated target landmarks, skin and person plans, complete physical results, diameter, canonical PreparedDefectParams and a private fingerprint. `prepareNativeTargetDefectParams` requires the same immutable target namespace/binding, shared detector and qualification policy, face.landmarks operation and exact retained plan/result identities. It reads all three canonical producers and repeats their complete physical proofs after compilation. The original public request/reference is never changed.

`projectNativeTargetPersonProtection` verifies target person plan/result fingerprint, exact canonical output/root/pixel identity, qualified model lock and source/canvas mapping. It copies the original person probability pixels into a permanent protected declaration without changing probabilities, thresholding or producing a replacement MaskRef. Existing bindNativeDefectFaceInputs and PrepareDefectParams preserve the established face geometry, skin and defect processing contract. No additional/manual roots are supplied by this automatic path.

`verifyNativeTargetDefectPreparation` recompiles Params from retained target proofs and compares the entire output and private fingerprint, including physical skin objects/content/pixel count. `recheckNativeTargetDefectPreparation` repeats all physical producer reads and full equality. `publishNativeTargetDefectParams` brackets existing accepted-version scoped publication with these checks, uses uuidv5(target_namespace, semantic.defect.params:reference), verifies exact digest and excludes output aliases of every declared input. Repeat publication must return the same immutable ref; revoked session or substituted evidence must refuse. Preparation grants no SQL consumer/admission authority.

Remaining required target defect stages: dedicated dependency packets and scheduler locks; qualified CPU task/inventory and saved execution profile; blocked begin, exact registration, atomic seal; canonical worker output guard; physical result/protection coverage, finalization, saved recovery/automatic advancement; original transfer admission. Passing preparation/publication does not accept defects.propose, V27 or photographic model quality. No migration is needed for this physical preflight, which uses existing target producer readers and accepted-version Params publication.

### V27 target defect CPU qualification

`nativeTargetDefectQualification` binds the complete target preparation fingerprint to the exact current server policy, physical qualification report, installed CPU costs and private fingerprint. `prepareNativeTargetDefectQualification` repeats all target dependency proofs, reads current policy and requires equality with retained target detector policy. It selects defects.propose version 1 CPU policy and verifies an explicit server-selected report through readDefectExecutionQualification. Nonzero VRAM costs refuse. Report peaks must be covered by installed costs; no client report or changed policy may replace server authority.

`targetDefectQualificationDigest` validates retained preparation and owner/session/project/bucket scope, serializes the exact report pin and hashes full expected policy, metrics, immutable object metadata and costs with preparation/policy. `verifyNativeTargetDefectQualification` refuses any changed fingerprint or retained fields. `recheckNativeTargetDefectQualification` repeats physical target producers, current report ownership/object pins and live policy before downstream use. No object IO is allowed inside later SQL consumer/admission transactions; this preflight is not their substitute. Qualification replay must remain exact; changes to preparation, report, costs, GPU cost, policy, session, object version or measured peaks refuse. Revoked session refuses and restored authority must pass.

This reuses the existing qualification report contract and fixed API queries; no migration or producer algorithm change. Dedicated target task/inventory, dependency locks and task admission remain required before activation. Report validation over fixture ML doubles is not scientific GPU/photo-quality acceptance.

### V27 target defect task and immutable inventory

`compileNativeTargetDefectInventory` first verifies dedicated target physical preparation. Params SHA/byte count must equal the retained canonical payload; the person pixel pin must equal the qualified canonical target person pixels. Source WorkingImage/array pins come from the retained target detector source; landmark and skin pins come from their exact target physical results. Zero-face payloads omit unused landmark/skin files. The shared assembleNativeDefectPinnedInventory only assembles byte inventory: unique declared refs, no Params alias, exact one-to-one candidates, valid digest/key/version/size/media, supported contracts, deterministic UUID order and complete coverage. Source and target callers retain separate producer authority; no fake source plan is constructed.

`compileNativeTargetDefectTask` validates dedicated target qualification and CPU costs, derives run uuidv5(target_namespace, semantic.defect.prepare:reference), workflow uuidv5(run, workflow) and task uuidv5(workflow, defects.propose:0). Existing CompileSemanticDefectTask preserves the native worker contract, CPU resource profile, Params/input digest and bounded three-ref bootstrap with complete indexed closure. Capacity below required RAM/scratch/output refuses. The private task fingerprint includes full draft task, target preparation/qualification fingerprints, immutable Params/person pins and canonical ordered entries/sizes.

`prepareNativeTargetDefectTask` repeats target qualification and complete physical dependencies before lookup. It requires exact published declared input/manual-root lists, namespace Params ownership, physical canonical Params bytes and a MaskPixels lookup under the exact canonical person MaskRef. It recompiles the frozen task, repeats physical producers/live policy/report, Params object/pixel lookup and Params bytes after IO. All IO precedes later SQL transactions. Repeat task compilation must reproduce the fingerprint and memory transport; altered Params/person/size/version/media or insufficient capacity refuse.

Task compilation does not persist a draft, publish live transport, reserve quota, queue or run target defects. Dedicated target defect blocked draft/compiler, dependency locks, registration, admission/seal, worker canonical guards, result/finish and saved recovery remain required before V27 acceptance. No migration or image algorithm change is made by this compiler slice.

### V27 target defect blocked draft plan compiler

`nativeTargetDefectDraftPlan` retains actual defects.propose target anchor, fully owned target task preparation, immutable shell and private fingerprint. `compileNativeTargetDefectDraftPlan` verifies dedicated target preparation/qualification and task fingerprint, extracts exact CPU resource estimates/report/costs/reserved bytes into nativePlanRecoveryMetadata, recompiles the frozen task and compares full task, fingerprint, canonical entries and sizes. Nonzero VRAM or reserve below output plus 8 MiB refuses.

The actual defects.propose DAG anchor is compiled from original request/key/intent/snapshot and persisted target manifest/ordinal; public reference is never replaced. Run uuidv5(namespace, semantic.defect.prepare:reference), workflow/task IDs, CPU operation and Params must match the frozen task. Canonical v27_target_defect_prepare config stores original parent/request/session, target ordinal/namespace/stage/manifest SHA, direct target binding/intent/snapshot, namespace Params document/ref, task/input digest/policy, preparation/qualification fingerprints, complete input entries/sizes, three sorted direct refs, diameter, and exact person/landmarks/skin run/task/result/output/Params lineage. Recovery retains explicit qualification report and saved costs/estimates, with no ordinary continuation or substituted current capacity.

The shell has one defects.propose CPU task, no edges, original target snapshot/project/actor, exact config/policy SHA and reserved bytes; key is target-defect-prep:<run>. `targetDefectPlanDigest` verifies actual target anchor, complete preparation fingerprint and qualification scope/report/metrics/object/cost fingerprint before hashing task/key/shell. `verifyNativeTargetDefectPlanSeal` repeats owned preparation validation and refuses cancellation. Compiler replay must reproduce full fingerprint/config. Changes to namespace/stage/config/shell/budget/task/qualification costs or peaks/prepared Params/person pin/inventory refuse, including insufficient reserve.

This is an in-memory blocked plan, not persisted draft creation. Dedicated scheduler dependency locks, blocked begin transaction, registration, seal, worker execution/output guard, physical result/finalization and saved recovery remain required. No new migration or production state change is included. V27 acceptance remains open.

### V27 target defect canonical dependency barrier

`nativeTargetDefectDependencies` retains dedicated target preparation, exact persisted transports for person/detector/landmarks/parser/skin, complete target person and landmark admission packets, physical completed-skin packet and a private fingerprint. `prepareNativeTargetDefectDependencies` repeats complete physical target preparation, compiles original-parent-bound person/landmark envelopes, physically rereads skin while constructing its completed evidence, then repeats physical preparation. All object reads happen before opening a consumer transaction. Packets are bounded valid JSON and the fingerprint includes full transport values and all three packets.

`lockNativeTargetDefectDependenciesInTransaction` requires caller-owned original parent advisory lock and rollback on every error. Existing target locks enforce scheduler role, tenant scope, current original request/session/policy, exact sealed transport and canonical result ownership/pins. Skin uses the physical evidence packet through its fixed SQL consumer barrier. No object IO, discovery, publication, task creation, queueing or lifecycle transition occurs inside this helper.

This canonical consumer barrier does not independently require completed run state for every producer: the upcoming blocked begin/admission must additionally lock and require completed person/landmarks/skin producer runs and current CPU defect qualification in the same transaction. Consumer proof alone cannot admit target defects. Tests must exercise canonical repeat, altered packets/preparation, syntactically valid rehashed invalid skin packet at the SQL boundary, and revoked/restored session. Full target defect blocked persistence/registration/seal/execution/result/finalization/recovery and V27 acceptance remain open. No new SQL migration is included in this barrier slice.

### V27 target defect completed dependencies and blocked begin / 00950

Migration 00950 adds scheduler-only SECURITY DEFINER lock_semantic_target_defect_completed_dependencies, owned by photo_fn_owner with fixed search_path and no new table grants/backfill. It accepts exactly three ordered producer locators (person, landmarks, skin), requires current editor/session/tenant scope, exact namespace-derived run/workflow/task/stage IDs, completed sealed uncancelled v2 runs, succeeded uncancelled version-1 tasks and canonical task_results at the same attempt/fence. Shared parent/request/manifest/ordinal/source/intent/snapshot must match across producers. Rows are held FOR SHARE in person → landmarks → skin order. This is additive to complete canonical consumer proof; standalone completed-state checking cannot grant admission.

lockNativeTargetDefectDependenciesInTransaction now calls this completed-state barrier after all dedicated canonical locks. The earlier limitation that completed state was not independently checked is closed by 00950; qualification remains a separate transaction gate.

beginNativeTargetDefectDraftInTransaction requires caller-owned original parent advisory lock and rollback on every error. It verifies/recompiles the full target draft plan, matches dependency preparation fingerprint, recompiles actual defects.propose anchor from original request/manifest and checks color profile. In the same transaction it locks current target entry, complete canonical/completed dependencies, exact CPU qualification report and full policy snapshot, then exact namespace Params object pins through existing accepted-version fencing. All object IO and report/proof generation happen before this transaction. Only then may BeginAdmissionInTransaction persist the single blocked task. Exact replay permits created/pending only and must retain draft/unsealed/blocked state. It neither registers task input transport nor activates/queues target defects.

Tests require all three completed producers to pass; changing each producer run to running must refuse until restored, revoked session must refuse, and first begin/replay must remain draft/unsealed/blocked with no canonical result. Forward upgrade00899→00950 must preserve tenant metadata and existing function identity/security. Registration/seal/worker/result/finalization/recovery and whole V27 acceptance remain required.

### V27 target defect input transport publication and binding

publishNativeTargetDefectDraftTransport validates the complete dedicated target draft plan, exact namespace-derived run/workflow/task, one CPU defect task/no edges, and equality of shell task with frozen task. It publishes existing FrozenSemanticDefectTask indexed closure only after blocked child commit, freezes full registration proof and repeats target plan verification. Returned Params/task/input digest must match the frozen values. The helper itself is a transport compiler/publisher; it does not replace live owner authority or registration.

publishOwnedNativeTargetDefectTransport brackets publication with current complete target preparation and CPU qualification checks, recompiles the defects.propose anchor from the original parent request and immutable target manifest, and returns no proof on failed postcheck. Frozen BindPublishedTransport and full proof compare Params/input digest/profile/manifest/chunks/ordered rows/direct refs. Malformed manifest/chunk refs and mismatched count/entries digest must refuse. Valid-looking foreign transport UUIDs can serialize to a distinct binding; serialization grants no object ownership or activation authority. Exact manifest/chunk/backend-document ownership must be checked by the dedicated SQL registration boundary. SQL registration must later repeat blocked begin, exact target dependencies/qualification/Params and dedicated target registration state in one transaction; publication alone never creates v2_task_inputs or queues a run.

Physical tests publish immutable backend-owned manifest/chunks for the committed blocked target draft, prove exact replay and binding, and independently read every object from S3 with pinned version/SHA/size and exact manifest/chunk refs/ordered entries. SQL independently confirms zero registered task inputs and zero queued outbox. Target SQL registration, seal, native worker/result/finalization/recovery and V27 acceptance remain required. No migration or photo-processing algorithm changes are included.

### V27 target defect input registration / 00951

Migration00951 adds private lock_v27_target_defect_graph_scope and semantic_target_defect_preparation_registration_state plus scheduler-only strict semantic_target_defect_registration_binding_state. All are SECURITY DEFINER, owned by photo_fn_owner with fixed search_path; no table privilege expansion. Target scope repeats original detector/request/session/project/immutable manifest, actual defects stage5 DAG requiring person/landmarks/skin, deterministic namespace/run/task/Params, blocked CPU shell and completed producer barrier.

Registration state requires exact saved Params/input digest/profile, canonical rows/direct refs/count, Params and WorkingImage cardinality, complete unique input closure and canonical rows SHA. Before returning missing it verifies every manifest/chunk ref belongs to the exact ready backend document scope (tenant/run/task/purpose) and ready artifact SHA/version/bytes/bucket/media/expiry. Valid-looking foreign transport UUIDs therefore refuse before registration. Missing means no stored input rows, untouched profile and empty bootstrap. Replayed means every ordered row, transport ordinal/contract, manifest/chunk/direct ref and augmented profile exactly agrees and closure_ready is true. Partial state returns conflict and is never repaired.

Existing v2_pending_shell_matches is replaced in place with an additive target defect CPU registered-shell branch; prior branches are preserved and function OID/security stay stable. registerNativeTargetDefectInputsInTransaction verifies dedicated plan and frozen binding, repeats full blocked begin/dependency/qualification/Params authority under caller-owned original parent lock, accepts exact replay, registers only missing state through existing RegisterInTransaction, and requires final replayed state. Caller commits/rolls back; no object IO or activation occurs in this transaction.

Tests must prove missing→replayed→replayed, foreign manifest conflict both before and after registration, revoked-session refusal and exact draft/unsealed/blocked final state. Independent SQL/S3 verifies complete registered row count and closure readiness with zero queued outbox. Upgrade00899→00951 preserves metadata and function identity/security. Seal/native worker/result/finalization/saved recovery and full V27 acceptance remain required.

### V27 target defects: отдельный admission envelope

`marshalNativeTargetDefectAdmissionEvidence` собирает schema_version=1 с точным
config_text target-плана, полными target person/landmarks/completed-skin пакетами,
policy, CPU qualification expectation/report pin, Params object pin и registration
binding. Перед сериализацией повторно проверяет полный seal плана, recompilation,
seal всех dependency packets, совпадение preparation и исходного parent authority
в person/landmarks. Params pin связывает SHA, размер фактического JSON, bucket/key,
не-null object version и application/json. Пакет ограничен 32MiB; cancellation
возвращает ошибку без пакета. Метод не выполняет object IO, SQL или activation.

Тип `nativeTargetDefectAdmissionEvidence` отделён от reference-envelope:
`person` содержит полный target admission packet. Подмена этого поля reference-only
person authority запрещена. Seal dependency-пакета обеспечивает целостность в Go,
но не заменяет повторный completed-skin/canonical-result SQL lock. Registration
binding описывает transport; право владения объектами подтверждается 00951.
Следующий обязательный этап — dedicated SQL admission и seal с повторением всех
зависимостей, qualification, policy, Params pins, registration и quota в одной TX.

### V27 target defects: SQL admission barrier 00952

`lockNativeTargetDefectAdmissionInTransaction` собирает dedicated target envelope,
требует роль photo_scheduler и выставляет transaction-local app.tenant_id перед
вызовом `lock_semantic_target_defect_admission_evidence`. Caller удерживает
original parent advisory lock и откатывает TX при любой ошибке. Object IO нет.
SQL повторяет immutable original manifest/target DAG, completed producer runs,
точные canonical result refs, общие source/intent/snapshot/namespace/request,
актуальные qualification/policy/Params pins, транспортную регистрацию и квоту.
Успешный admission удерживает locks до caller commit; не меняет blocked/draft,
не создаёт queued outbox, не заменяет dedicated seal и native result admission.

### V27 target defects: atomic seal 00953

`sealNativeTargetDefectInTransaction` сериализует полный dedicated target packet
и вызывает `seal_semantic_target_defect_preparation` через общий строгий ticket
adapter. Caller удерживает original parent advisory lock; никакого object IO
внутри TX. SQL объединяет admission, exact native activation, единственный
queued event/outbox и durable 202 receipt. Повтор при revoked session запрещён,
несмотря на существующий receipt. Sealed replay повторяет current dependency,
policy, qualification, Params и registration authority, без повторной активации.
Worker claim/start, native canonical-result guard и finish/recovery остаются
отдельными обязательными этапами: queued/ready не означает completed V27.

### V27 target defects: native worker execution 00954

Target defects использует существующий CPU `operations.defects.propose.execute`,
не отдельный упрощённый алгоритм. Полный indexed input transport читается через
mTLS gateway; outputs проходят upload verification и dedicated target SQL guard.
Повтор commit_success возвращает тот же canonical result без повторного учёта
ресурсов. После worker commit task=succeeded, run=running до отдельного finish.
Defect labels PNG соответствует label NPY; labels на protected pixels равны0.
Проверка с детерминированными upstream ML fixtures не заменяет real-photo/GPU
qualification. Finish/result catalog, recovery и автоматическое продвижение
target pipeline остаются обязательными до завершения V27.

### V27 target defects: canonical metadata finish 00955

`finishNativeTargetDefectInTransaction` принимает полный target plan/dependencies,
transport и ожидаемые canonical result ID/пять ordered refs. Требует scheduler
role, transaction-local tenant и original parent lock у caller; SQL повторяет
весь sealed admission и canonical-output provenance до release quota/complete.
Этот метод завершает metadata канонической worker-задачи. Он не объявляет
report/candidates физически прочитанными downstream consumer: для передачи
semantic.transfer необходимы отдельные target result catalog и byte-verifier.
Completed replay и повторное чтение исходного 202 ticket не активируют задачу.

### V27 target defects: physical result verification 00956

`nativeTargetDefectResultCatalog` и `nativeTargetDefectResultObjects` сохраняют
отдельную target authority; общий decoder используется только для строгого DTO.
`readCompletedNativeTargetDefect` до/после object IO повторяет target preparation,
qualification, exact catalog и шесть pins. Читает реальные Params/proposal/report
с byte SHA/size проверкой, связывает report с target WorkingImage/Params/proposal,
проверяет label PNG against NPY, отсутствие labels на protection и faces masks.
Declared permanent mask должна точно совпадать с исходной target-person маской,
с protected=true. Streaming `VerifyProbabilityMaskCoverage` подтверждает полное
покрытие исходных вероятностей в protection output без threshold подмены.
Неверные bytes/links/catalog/dependencies, revoked session или cancellation не
возвращают evidence. Object IO выполняется вне admission transaction. Возвращённый
result ещё требует downstream SQL dependency lock перед commit потребителя;
saved recovery, automatic target advance и transfer handoff остаются обязательны.

### V27 target defects: completed consumer authority 00957

`marshalNativeTargetDefectCompletedEvidence` повторно читает все physical result
bytes и current dependencies вне TX, сравнивает complete evidence с переданным
результатом и включает dedicated target admission plus six ordered file pins.
Общий completed DTO является только форматом сериализации; source-only план
не создаётся. `lockNativeCompletedTargetDefectInTransaction` заново проверяет
полный target plan/recompilation, scheduler role и tenant, затем вызывает00957
в original parent transaction. Caller откатывает любую ошибку. Packet сам по
себе не даёт прав: SQL повторяет текущие policy/qualification/dependencies,
registration и canonical outputs, требует completed run. Этот lock необходимо
включить в downstream graph commit; его наличие отдельно не означает завершение
transfer handoff или автоматического recovery pipeline.

### V27 target defects: saved plan/transport recovery 00958

`readNativeTargetDefectSaved` проверяет actual target anchor, namespace run/task,
canonical config SHA и допустимую stage phase. `restoreNativeTargetDefectPlan`
читает сохранённые diameter/recovery/report/costs/resource limits, заново получает
physical target person/landmarks/skin и current qualification, использует ранее
опубликованный Params ref без PUT и компилирует exact frozen task/plan. Проверяет
anchor seal, config SHA, input digest, task/profile/Params и повторный current
saved lookup после IO. Текущие capacity defaults не подставляются.
`recoverNativeTargetDefectTransport` в original parent TX повторяет target anchor
и читает только saved registered transport через00958, затем bind к frozen task.
Saved metadata — locators, а не capability: revoked scope или несовпадение любого

A second transaction must have a different pg_backend_pid and uses lock_timeout1s. Its mutation must fail with SQLSTATE55P03 while readiness transaction remains open. After both transactions roll back, a new writer repeats the identical mutation with the same timeout and must update exactly one row; this write also rolls back. This verifies lock retention through transaction lifetime for those concrete rows, not just a sequential reread.

A separate rollback transaction installs an already expired detector lease using the isolated fixture owner, invokes the real scheduler-only completed handoff recorder and requires false. Owner then reads done/token and requires done=false and the same token, proving this refusal did not retire or clear scheduling state. The native pipeline still independently checks ready tasks and exactly one queued event/outbox. No migration or production authority changes are introduced by these tests. Natural time expiry during a transaction, concurrent policy revocation, commit ambiguity, deadlock analysis across mixed lifecycle operations and full V27 acceptance remain independent requirements.


## V27 — natural TTL expiry inside handoff transaction

HANDOFF_LOCKS also runs two rollback-only natural-clock scenarios against the actual scheduler recorder. A non-reference target protection root is assigned a future three-second expires_at, or the original detector is assigned a future three-second owned recovery lease. The other lease remains live for one minute in the root case. The same open transaction must first observe positive automatic readiness and a live lease, wait with a context-cancellable timer, then call record_v27_native_initial_pipeline_handoff as photo_scheduler. Completion must return false and an owner read must retain done=false and the exact lease token. No timestamp is rewritten after the positive baseline. This distinguishes wall-clock checks from transaction-stable now() and complements row-lock tests: locks do not prevent time passing. Each transaction rolls back. The native Python harness requires the CLOCK_EXPIRY_REFUSED marker, so a missing scenario cannot silently pass.

This check does not establish arbitrary TTL timing interleavings or qualification-policy revocation safety. Review of00963 shows target source/protection/producer liveness checks but no explicit complete qualification-policy snapshot revalidation at handoff. Existing admission/seal policy checks are insufficient evidence for this later transition. Policy revocation and its concurrent lock behavior remain open requirements; no whole-module acceptance follows from TTL probes.


## V27 — policy snapshot at handoff, migration00964

The five-argument scheduler-only `record_v27_native_initial_pipeline_handoff(tenant,detector,token,outcome,policy_text)` receives the original canonical policy catalog from the independently restored detector plan. `SQLNativeInitialPipelineRecovery.ProcessOne` passes this owned text with the derived outcome; startup requires the new overload and its EXECUTE grant before claiming work. Non-completed outcomes need no policy snapshot. Manual and legacy parent completion retain their existing branches.

For completed automatic all-native parents, SQL first retains the existing tenant/usage/parent/lease lock order. It computes SHA256 over the supplied UTF8 text and requires the exact original detector workflow policy SHA, then calls the existing full catalog snapshot barrier and equality check. Original reference defect and every non-reference target defect workflow must retain that same full catalog SHA under SHARE locks. The parent transfer policy uses a distinct fingerprint; its saved qualification pin is independently revalidated through the existing selected policy/import/report/calibration lock function. Existing00963 source/protection/producer liveness and original canonical-result checks remain mandatory, followed by the base recorder's live lease/fence checks. Refusal leaves scheduling state unchanged. No caller-supplied current catalog can replace the original saved workflow digest.

The four-argument overload refuses completed automatic all-native parents even with a valid lease. It continues to serve waiting/retry/failed and manual/legacy branches, preventing bypass of the snapshot gate without rewriting existing saved configs or workflow hashes. Both overloads are SECURITY DEFINER with explicit search_path, photo_fn_owner ownership, PUBLIC revoked and scheduler-only application EXECUTE. No tables or new DML grants are introduced.

The global qualification-policy writer trigger now runs BEFORE STATEMENT for INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE. Writers acquire the exclusive advisory barrier before touching policy rows; readers acquire its shared counterpart before selected policy row locks. This removes the prior policy-row/advisory-lock inversion. It is not a proof against every mixed lifecycle deadlock. Roll out00964 before updated scheduler startup; old scheduler completion of all-native work will be refused until upgraded, preserving the queued graph and lease retry rather than silently accepting a weaker handoff. No production migration is applied by these checks.

HANDOFF_LOCKS requires rollback-only recorder refusals after revoking admission for each of the six catalog operations, as well as empty/malformed/unbound snapshot text. Every refusal must retain done=false and the exact token. A positive five-argument handoff must retire the lease, while the legacy four-argument call must refuse. A distinct policy writer session must get55P03 under a one-second lock timeout while that handoff transaction is open, and the identical mutation must succeed after rollback. The Python harness requires both new policy markers. Source/root/producer mutation and natural-TTL probes call the five-argument boundary so policy requirements cannot hide a liveness regression.

These checks do not establish full V27 acceptance. Upstream imported qualification revocation beyond the transfer pin, original reference exact locator-version cases, pending target-stage job progression, partial transport publication repair, commit ambiguity, crash/restart, GC/purge, scale and real-photo/GPU quality remain separately required.

### V27 explicit child-status retry UI — head00974

`retrySemanticChild(client,tenant,run): boolean` now permits an explicit re-read of an existing failed shared channel. It refuses nonexistent, foreign, disposed, pending, healthy and terminal channels. `Channel.retry` aborts the old controller, creates a fresh controller, clears the old timer, synchronously resets failed state for all subscribers and starts exactly one poll. Reset occurs before IO, so rapid repeat actions cannot overlap requests. `poll` owns a captured controller for its request, so disposal/auth scope changes still suppress late replies. HTTP401/403/404/500 and network failure continue to stop automatic polling and clear stale progress; manual retry is a new authorized server read, not an authority cache or automatic permission retry loop.

`SemanticChildProgress` displays a localized Retry button only while the shared status read has failed. A successful manual response clears the error/button, resumes normal polling or fires the current terminal callback once. Repeated refusal restores the error/button and remains stopped until another user action. Background/dispose continues to unsubscribe/abort; retry cannot create an unobserved channel. Existing client/tenant/run scoping, shared callback de-duplication and no persistent cache remain intact. EN/RU/BG failure text no longer instructs the user to use a different refresh control; the existing localized retry key is reused.

Current photographer Vitest451PASS/7SKIP (458total); tsc --noEmit PASS. New tests cover five failure kinds, shared/rapid retry, pending/foreign-scope refusal, repeated403 without auto-loop and disposal during manual retry, plus actual button-to-completed DOM behavior. ### V28 keyed preliminary draft service contract (2026-10-09)

CreateKeyedDraft(ctx, authenticatedIdentity, key, RelightDraftIntent) accepts snapshot UUID, ordinal, subject kind and authored Params only. Tenant/project are bound by RelightSubmissionService; UserID and SessionID must be nonzero, key must pass idempotency.ValidKey, canceled context refuses before database use. Run identity is UUIDv5(namespace=tenant, name="V28.Relight.CreateDraft:"+projectUUID+":"+actorUUID+":"+key); workflow is existing UUIDv5(run,"workflow"). This separates relight draft keys from generic V2.Admit keys. Returned RelightSubmissionRequest owns Params bytes. CreateDraft persists exact full request/config through01012; identical draft replay succeeds, changed intent/session or stale authorization refuses. No request hash is mixed into run IDs, so changed content cannot silently create a second run under the same key. This preliminary contract does not promise replay after graph admission; durable queued receipt and public response idempotency still need explicit route orchestration.

### Frozen import evidence transport

`FrozenInterchangeImportEvidence` — scheduler-owned JSON declared input (до 8 MiB), ID UUIDv5(import task, `interchange:evidence:1`). ### Executable import Params evidence pin

`V2InterchangeImportParams.evidence` обязателен. Порядок publication: Plan → FrozenInterchangeImportEvidence → Params. Полный closure и direct roots включают exact evidence ref. Legacy four-field storage helpers не являются admission contract; SQL01337 отклоняет их. Final activation/adoption обязаны повторить current proof gate.


### Portable bundle background mapping
BundleManifest v1 has optional backgrounds (omitted for legacy archives; when present 1..5000 entries). Each BundleBackground contains exact StyleV2 ArtifactRef style, portable_asset_id (nonempty UTF-8 <=120 bytes, trimmed), path and sha256. The referenced inventory style must match UUID/SHA/StyleV2; the file must match path/SHA/StyleBackgroundAsset. Duplicate (style UUID, portable ID) mappings are rejected. Mapping declarations alone do not authorize assets: native bytes and frozen/current source authority remain mandatory. Export/import codec integration is required to preserve portable IDs across tenant remapping.

### V29 multi replacement review discovery and navigation

GET `/api/v1/tenants/{t}/projects/{id}/reviews/{review}/multi-replacements/{original}` requires editor authority, one canonical `decision_hash`, and exactly one `operation` (`optical.panorama` or `optical.focus_stack`). Returns a closed MultiReplacementReviewScope containing current mapped intent, deterministic replacement run/workflow/task and original identity; stale/unavailable mapping produces an unavailable response. This is read-only metadata, never admission or Apply authority.

GET `/api/v1/tenants/{t}/reviews/{review}/multi-replacements` requires the current rejected negative review decision hash and optional UUID `after`. Reads immutable invalidations in UUID order, at most100 items; each item includes explicit operation and nullable current scope. `next_after` is the last returned original task only when another item exists. Both endpoints return `Cache-Control: no-store`.

Original review exposes current multi replacement links. Mounted `/multi-replacements/[original]` reloads discovery and live workflow, validates replacement namespace and exact frame order, then uses existing content-verified proposal, preview, independent quality and explicit retained Apply. Durable Apply recovery is scoped to origin/user/tenant/project/replacement producer. No collection selection is inferred. Tenant/project/review/decision/operation/API changes cancel old requests. Acceptance remains unproven until positive tenant isolation, pagination, revoked/mapped-source, mixed frame, content/witness, warning confirmation, ambiguous Apply recovery and mounted navigation scenarios run on the current build. Tests and migrations authored in this increment are NOT RUN/NOT APPLIED.


### CorrectionPlan snapshot ordinals in render/export

CorrectionPlan.items[].ordinal retains the original InputSnapshot ordinal, including scene groups with gaps or a first ordinal greater than zero (for example `[2,7]`). Require strictly increasing unique ordinals in0..4999 and unique asset-version bindings; never renumber the group. ValidateScenePlan/scoped provenance must additionally prove each ordinal/binding against the producer snapshot and group. JSON occurrence paths use the item array index (`items[1]` for snapshot ordinal7); these are distinct coordinates. CurrentCorrection selects by the exact source binding, not by array offset or presumed contiguous ordinal.


## V30: локальный ZIP и восстановление загрузки

`pickInterchangeArchive` выполняет `sweepArchiveCache` перед нативным picker. Picker запрашивает один ZIP с `copyToCacheDirectory=true`; возвращённая копия должна находиться внутри `Paths.cache`, без query/fragment. Оригиналы за пределами кеша отклоняются без удаления. После проверки размера `stageArchiveCache` переносит копию в `Paths.cache/photoeditor-interchange-v1/<UUID>.zip` и регистрирует активный URI до асинхронного хеширования. Имя оригинала сохраняется только в upload request; оно не определяет локальный путь.

`sweepArchiveCache` синхронно удаляет только прямые File entries этого каталога с именем UUID.zip, отсутствующие в process-local active set. Не удаляет каталоги, вложенные пути, неизвестные имена и активные выборы. После перезапуска active set пуст: прежние копии удаляются при следующем нативном выборе; продолжение требует повторно выбрать исходный ZIP и заново вычислить SHA. Исторические копии общего picker-кеша не удаляются по предположению об ownership. Ошибка sweep прерывает новый выбор, сохраняя возможность явного повтора.

`releaseArchiveSource` сначала запрещает дальнейшие чтения выбранного source. Cache release снимает active claim и удаляет копию; при ошибке callback остаётся доступным для повтора, а следующий sweep также может удалить неактивный файл. Ошибка подготовки вместе с ошибкой очистки возвращает AggregateError с обеими причинами. Отмена после возврата picker очищает полученную cache-копию.

`executeArchiveUpload` восстанавливает точные сохранённые ETag без повторного чтения и PUT этих частей. Перед и после каждого асинхронного действия проверяет отмену и source metadata. Подписанный URL проверяется при получении и повторно после чтения части непосредственно перед PUT: HTTP(S), без userinfo/fragment/CR/LF/NUL, максимум32768 UTF-8 байт, expiry строго дальше5 секунд и не дальше15 минут. Эта проверка envelope не подтверждает подпись и не заменяет серверную authority.

Сохранённый stage queued/aborting разрешает только чтение статуса. Подтверждение Complete записывается в durable journal до запроса статуса; ошибка записи не разрешает переход к импорту. `onReady` допускается только после свежего ready-ответа и повторного чтения неизменного durable attempt, при живом экране, не более одного раза. Явный restart после server-confirmed aborted очищает selection/status до освобождения кеша: ошибка локального удаления не возвращает уже сброшенный журнал в работу.

Эти правила являются контрактом реализации. Наличие функций и unit-сценариев не подтверждает нативное поведение файловой системы, завершённость полного V30 lifecycle или прохождение тестов.

Перед возобновлением сохранённого uploading checkpoint `runArchiveUpload` читает свежий scoped status с journal fence до/после IO. Только state=uploading с будущим expires_at допускает продолжение; остальные состояния возвращаются без новых Parts/PUT/Complete. Это preflight, а не разрешение обходить повторную серверную authority при каждой операции.

`prepareArchiveCache(signal)` выполняется также при входе в маршрут загрузки до загрузки principal и показа формы: на native удаляет только неактивные owned ZIP, на web не выполняет filesystem IO. Отмена проверяется до/после подготовки; ошибка очистки не допускает показ готового uploader. После аварийного перезапуска очистка больше не требует нового выбора файла: достаточно открыть маршрут импорта архива.


### V25 explicit RAW development policy (2026-10-12)

`RAWDevelopment` v1: `schema_version=1`, `demosaic=ahd|dcb`, `wb_source=camera|daylight|auto`, finite `exposure_ev` in[-2,3], `highlight_policy=clip|blend|reconstruct`, `decoder_version=rawpy-X.Y.Z/libraw-X.Y.Z` (max128 characters). Go `IngestItem.RAWDevelopment` freezes the policy into asset.decode Params. The complete policy, including exact decoder identity, participates in canonical Params hashing; historical nil policy is omitted in Go and retains the original decoding behavior. No migration rewrites historical Params or cached outputs.

Worker validates the installed rawpy/LibRaw version before opening RAW. Explicit camera WB requires available finite positive RGB gains; no implicit daylight substitution. Exposure maps to `exp_shift=2**EV`, with automatic brightness disabled and highlight preservation set explicitly to0; highlight modes map to LibRaw Clip/Blend/ReconstructDefault. Output remains linear sRGB16 before the existing LittleCMS work-profile conversion; input originals remain unchanged. Raster sources reject an attached RAW policy. AHD/DCB capability declarations still require trusted installation and real camera/quality qualification.

Current source implementation covers typed policy/compiler/task reconstruction/worker option forwarding only. Public request and immutable snapshot selection, client controls, trusted decoder qualification and real RAW/ICC/camera acceptance are not established by this increment. Unit tests are authored, not executed.

V25 ingest snapshot payload v2 extends the exact v1 fields with required non-null `raw_development`. V1 must omit it; explicit null cannot round-trip canonically. Reader validates version/policy pairing and policy values, then recompiles the full batch; raster suffixes cannot carry RAW policy. This is reader-side support only until upload admission, persisted immutable policy and all completion SQL gates are expanded together. Existing v1 snapshots are neither rewritten nor assigned implicit new settings.


V25 migration01618 persists nullable `upload_sessions.raw_development` with complete policy validation and an immutable-update trigger. User/device Begin normalize and own the typed policy before idempotency; legacy nil is SQL NULL. The scheduler-only `claim_upload_completion_v2` wraps the existing claim without replacing its signature and reads the immutable policy. Completion documents use snapshot v2 iff policy is present; finish/fail recheck the entire expected snapshot against locked upload filename/profile/policy. Guest uploads retain default v1. No historical rows are backfilled. Migration and integration scenarios are authored, not applied or executed; client/public contract and qualification work remain open.


V25 `rawdevelopment.VerifyCapability` reuses exact canonical qualification verification with trusted deployment `Expected` pins for asset.decode/1/cpu. Mandatory checks: raw.camera_matrix; raw.demosaic.ahd/dcb; raw.wb.camera/daylight/auto; raw.highlights.clip/blend/reconstruct; raw.exposure.bounds; raw.icc.roundtrip; raw.originals.unchanged. Caller must independently authenticate the issuer and physical evidence, and bind the trusted decoder_version to that exact installed runtime lock. Report-contained values never supply their own expected pins. Capability has private identity, an owned view and exact version matching. This verifier does not provision/import evidence, install a decoder, publish a public endpoint or recheck live SQL authority; those integrations remain required before UI enablement.

V25 RAW qualification source01619 locks the exact trusted qualification, ready report artifact and matching enabled operator policy under the caller transaction, requires current v2_editor_authorized and asset.decode/1/cpu. Source reads only its deployment-pinned bucket/report/version and bounded8MiB JSON, verifies actual report bytes through VerifyCapability, then rechecks current SQL authority and physical descriptor. It must be called inside the authenticated tenant transaction; a pool/autocommit lookup does not retain its locks through admission. Runtime installation and production catalogue/admission wiring remain distinct required steps. Tests/migrations not executed.

### Sidecar import archive contract (implementation increment 2026-10-12)

For `xmp`, ZIP inventory consists exclusively of JPEG/PNG/TIFF images and exactly one same-stem lowercase `.xmp` companion in the same relative directory for each image. `captureone_sidecar` permits TIFF images only with the same pairing rule; `.cos` and catalogs remain unsupported. Ambiguous stems (e.g. a.jpg plus a.tiff), unpaired files and case-fold duplicate paths refuse the archive. The existing verified ZIP limits and canonical image ingest apply; file extensions and inline contract labels do not establish image validity. XMP reads are bounded to1MiB each, normalized manifest/parsed documents to8MiB. Unsupported properties remain in retained imported XMP; MappingReport records their count and SHA of the canonical ordered property-name list. No unsupported development setting is silently applied. Source format is retained in SQL proof (historical no-format proofs mean bundle) and job projection by01771. Migrations01770/01771 are authored, NOT APPLIED; tests are authored, NOT RUN.

Sidecar ZIP may also include the optional `manifest.json` emitted by the PhotoEditor exporter. Its physical SHA/size/inventory must pass verify_bundle_manifest and match independently parsed image/XMP metadata. No recipes/masks/backgrounds are accepted in sidecar format. This preserves original portable entry identities; raw external ZIP without manifest receives deterministic bundle-scoped identities. The manifest itself is parser metadata, not an imported file.


### V23 holdout beauty composition and admission

Frozen baseline/candidate/approved renders apply the existing portrait.beauty multi-face stage after aligned color, with pinned source FaceObservations, exact canvas/transform, permanent protected mask and effective_beauty_params strength exactly once. Nonzero face effects skipped by production preparation refuse measurable holdout success. Other unconnected effects still refuse explicitly; no complete-renderer qualification is implied. Admission reserves 116 bytes per largest-frame pixel, 16 bytes per ROI pixel for up to64 ROIs capped at4194304 pixels each, 4 bytes per largest region-mask pixel aggregate, 512MiB transport/metric headroom and 2MiB NPY headroom. Total remains bounded by16GiB. This accounts for candidate retouch enabling beauty over a disabled base style. Authored tests are NOT RUN.

V23 beauty additionally requires pinned bisenet_face19 for nonempty source faces. Source work RGB converts to model sRGB before crop512/expansion1.35; Face19 labels use exact existing face.parse decode and confidence overlap selection, skin=class1. One scoped skin map is shared across baseline/candidate/approved. Missing parsing is an explicit refusal, never a fabricated mask. Full-frame parsing raises RAM admission to96 bytes per largest-frame pixel plus1GiB, with existing4GiB floor. Pixel parity and model/scope tests are authored, NOT RUN.

Holdout Face19 parsing retains production mean_confidence across source faces and refuses measurement below0.75. Detector face quality must never replace semantic parsing confidence. Per-face logits/warp buffers are released before holdout beauty composition.

V23 holdout beauty must not treat FaceObservations.quality as landmark confidence. It recovers FAN points/confidences from pinned source using the same preview2048, model sRGB conversion, crop256/expansion1.25 and coordinate mapping as session.describe; exact frozen point coordinates must reproduce. Missing model, mismatched coordinates or cancellation refuse before beauty. Face68 retains actual FAN confidence for production confidence gates. No confidence is synthesized.


### V30 archive Begin SDK retry boundary

Production objectstore transport limits an archive Begin invocation to one actual CreateMultipartUpload HTTP POST using a private call-local atomic token. SDK retry is refused before forwarding a second Create; rejected request bodies are closed. Completion, parts, discovery and other uploads keep their existing behavior. The token is not a durable receipt or evidence that an in-flight storage request terminated. Lost first response still keeps the existing Begin writer open; no retry/inspection may infer closure from empty listing or this local limit alone. SDK/physical tests are authored, NOT RUN.


### V24 Studio Agent signed part capability

Parts response upload_id must equal the persisted capture upload. Each exact requested part carries timezone-aware expires_at no later than15 minutes from current time. Validate before URL use and immediately before PUT; expiry within5 seconds is a retryable transport condition. Retry obtains a fresh capability for the same durable upload/part, retaining successful sibling ETags and the original Begin key. Wrong scope/malformed expiry/excessive horizon refuse as protocol errors. Device credentials never accompany storage PUT. ETags reject ASCII control characters. Authored scenarios are NOT RUN.


V23 training region assembly: TrainingRegionSource.LoadRegions reads the exact scoped frozen BaseStyle through the existing catalog/document reader. TrainingMaskSelector.SelectRegions discovers only registered event-closure roots, requires unique nonprotected same-canvas/transform confidence>=.75 masks per authored selector, then validates the selected root and pixels through TrainingMaskSource and SQLTrainingMaskProvenance. Missing or ambiguous selectors return ErrInvalidTrainingExample; invalid or duplicate root evidence refuses the draft. Repeated authored selectors share one verified immutable mask, preserving region order. SQLTrainingCaseFactory installs this source and TrainingCaseAssembler copies the bindings into TrainingRenderCase before freezing. No new model inference, synthetic mask or portable-ID lookup is permitted. Tests authored NOT RUN; SQL/worker physical qualification remains required.


V23 background asset transport: TrainingRenderCase.background_asset is optional inline FrozenBackgroundAsset; absence omits the field and preserves old canonical manifests. Presence requires full v2draft FrozenBackgroundAsset validation, exact recipe base_style and training work ICC. Frozen evidence owns assume_profile. Worker repeats style/ICC binding. This structural field grants no source authority: SQL style-asset authorization, encoded source closure/admission, resource calculation and provider installation must all be connected before asset background evaluation is enabled. The authored provider now opens one scoped normalized asset for all three comparison arms and requires an explicit decoder runtime verifier; missing or replaced installation refuses before composition. SQL migrations01772/01773, production qualification and deployment installation remain unapplied/unverified; authored wiring does not enable qualified production asset evaluation.


### V30 client Create and archive handoff execution ownership (authored 2026-10-12)

`executeInterchangeCreate` owns request/callback values before IO and serializes each durable journal key through `interchange-create-execution:` lock plus in-process queue. Persist the original idempotency key before POST; repeat exact canonical journal and current scope around transport. An expected retry key must already exist unchanged; missing/replaced retry never allocates a new operation. Return an acknowledged attempt only after durable ticket persistence and a final journal read after callbacks. Lost transport/ticket persistence responses retain the original pending key. Callback values are independent copies.

`openInterchangeCreate` uses the same execution queue, requires exact canonical acknowledged attempt and current scope, then invokes a captured synchronous callback with an owned ticket. It performs no POST and does not discard the journal. `openReadyArchiveUpload` similarly uses the archive upload/abort/reset execution lock, repeats fresh validated status and journal identity, reports owned current status so stale ready UI is withdrawn, and hands off an owned artifact only while still active and ready. Logout generation fences remain authoritative. These paths are authored with unit/DOM scenarios, NOT RUN; they do not establish native cross-tab, physical workflow or whole V30 acceptance.


V30 routed review uses `executeInterchangeReview` with a durable journal and `interchange-review-execution:` lock. Freeze request/callbacks before IO; reconcile exact kind/key/inspection/decisions, persist intent before decide/close POST, fence journal and scope around transport, persist canonical receipt before clear. Existing receipt permits cleanup-only retry. Recovery requires a current durable attempt; a missing record or changed intent must not be reconstructed by retry. Close adapter supports exact normalized OwnerAPI receipt and validated HTTP receipt, retaining canonical six-field journal representation. Stop waiting aborts client work while preserving pending/acknowledged intent; it is distinct from cancelling the server run. Error/stage/action locale switches must not change selection, journal identity or issue mutation requests. Scenarios authored NOT RUN; SQL/native and cross-tab acceptance remain open.


### V25 immutable RAW selection at re-pick (authored, tests not run)

The import picker compares a selected file to paused/error uploads using name, size, last-modified time and SHA-256, then also requires the exact frozen RAW development fields (including decoder version). A different policy, including switching between explicit and legacy development, refuses the re-pick rather than silently resuming an earlier Begin under changed controls or creating an ambiguous duplicate. Raster uploads compare no RAW policy. More than one matching resumable record refuses as ambiguous. Both conflicts have localized recovery guidance; the original durable record can still be resumed or cancelled through its row. This is client identity fencing; it does not establish decoder qualification or physical camera-image acceptance.
